part03/ch30/synth/soc30_bd.tcl · tcl · 원본 · Vivado 실행 통과
# (추가) 30장: SoC 복제본에 모터 안전 경로를 더하고 Bitstream·XSA까지 만든다.
# (변경) 안 A(2026-11-20): 출발점을 28장 SoC에서 21장 동결 주소맵 SoC(ch21/build/soc21_npu)로 바꾼다.
#   21장 주소·인터럽트는 바꾸지 않고, 빈 칸 0x4007_0000(64 KB)에 모터 GPIO(axi_gpio_motor, 듀얼:
#   채널 1 출력 = 명령 워드, 채널 2 입력 = 상태 워드)만 더한다. 모터 경로는 인터럽트를 쓰지 않는다(In4 예약 그대로 0).
#   p3_motor_safety_bd(BD 모듈 참조, GPIO와 같은 클록·peripheral_aresetn) → 외부 포트(비상정지·고장·과열·리밋 입력,
#   IN1·IN2·ENA·트립 출력). 사용자 Top: basys3_soc30_top(핀 연결만). 21장 결과물은 build_soc.sh가 cp -a로 복제한다.
# 사용: vivado -mode batch -source soc30_bd.tcl -tclargs <복제 xpr> <ch30 폴더> <출력 폴더> <jobs>
set xpr [lindex $argv 0]
set ch [file normalize [lindex $argv 1]]
set out [file normalize [lindex $argv 2]]
set jobs [lindex $argv 3]
set gpio_base 0x40070000
file mkdir $out

proc check {cond msg} {
  if {[uplevel 1 [list expr $cond]]} { puts "CHECK_PASS $msg" } else { puts "CHECK_FAIL $msg"; exit 1 }
}

open_project $xpr
foreach f [get_files -quiet -of_objects [get_filesets constrs_1]] { remove_files -fileset constrs_1 $f }
add_files -fileset constrs_1 -norecurse $ch/constraints/basys3_soc30.xdc
add_files -norecurse [list $ch/rtl/p3_motor_safety.v $ch/rtl/p3_motor_safety_bd.v $ch/rtl/basys3_soc30_top.v]
set_property is_enabled false [get_files basys3_soc21_top.v]
update_compile_order -fileset sources_1

set bd_file [get_files -filter {FILE_TYPE == "Block Designs"}]
open_bd_design $bd_file
puts "BD [current_bd_design]"
check {[llength [get_bd_cells -quiet ip06_seg7]] == 1 && [llength [get_bd_cells -quiet p3_npu_0]] == 1 && [llength [get_bd_cells -quiet axi_gpio_btn]] == 1} "21장 안 A SoC(NPU + BTN + SEG7)에서 시작"

set smc [get_bd_cells axi_smc]
check {[get_property CONFIG.NUM_MI $smc] == 7} "SmartConnect NUM_MI 7 (21장 안 A)"
set_property CONFIG.NUM_MI 8 $smc
set g [create_bd_cell -type ip -vlnv xilinx.com:ip:axi_gpio:2.0 axi_gpio_motor]
set_property -dict [list CONFIG.C_IS_DUAL 1 CONFIG.C_GPIO_WIDTH 32 CONFIG.C_ALL_OUTPUTS 1 CONFIG.C_DOUT_DEFAULT 0x00000000 \
  CONFIG.C_GPIO2_WIDTH 32 CONFIG.C_ALL_INPUTS_2 1] $g
connect_bd_intf_net [get_bd_intf_pins axi_smc/M07_AXI] [get_bd_intf_pins axi_gpio_motor/S_AXI]
set clk_net [get_bd_nets -of_objects [get_bd_pins axi_gpio_0/s_axi_aclk]]
set rst_net [get_bd_nets -of_objects [get_bd_pins axi_gpio_0/s_axi_aresetn]]
connect_bd_net -net $clk_net [get_bd_pins axi_gpio_motor/s_axi_aclk]
connect_bd_net -net $rst_net [get_bd_pins axi_gpio_motor/s_axi_aresetn]

set m [create_bd_cell -type module -reference p3_motor_safety_bd motor_safety_0]
connect_bd_net -net $clk_net [get_bd_pins motor_safety_0/clk]
connect_bd_net -net $rst_net [get_bd_pins motor_safety_0/resetn]
connect_bd_net [get_bd_pins axi_gpio_motor/gpio_io_o] [get_bd_pins motor_safety_0/cmd_i]
connect_bd_net [get_bd_pins motor_safety_0/status_o] [get_bd_pins axi_gpio_motor/gpio2_io_i]
foreach {pin port} {estop_ok_i estop_ok estop_btn_i estop_btn fault_i fault otemp_i otemp lim_f_i lim_f lim_r_i lim_r
                    in1_o in1 in2_o in2 ena_o ena trip_o trip} {
  make_bd_pins_external -name $port [get_bd_pins motor_safety_0/$pin]
}
assign_bd_address -target_address_space /microblaze_riscv_0/Data [get_bd_addr_segs axi_gpio_motor/S_AXI/Reg] \
  -offset $gpio_base -range 64K
validate_bd_design
save_bd_design

set segs [get_bd_addr_segs -of_objects [get_bd_addr_spaces /microblaze_riscv_0/Data]]
set rows {}
foreach s $segs {
  lappend rows [list [get_property NAME $s] [get_property OFFSET $s] [get_property RANGE $s]]
  puts "ADDR [get_property NAME $s] [get_property OFFSET $s] [get_property RANGE $s]"
}
set overlap 0
foreach a $rows {
  foreach b $rows {
    if {[lindex $a 0] ne [lindex $b 0]} {
      set a0 [expr {[lindex $a 1]}]; set a1 [expr {$a0 + [lindex $a 2]}]
      set b0 [expr {[lindex $b 1]}]; set b1 [expr {$b0 + [lindex $b 2]}]
      if {$a0 < $b1 && $b0 < $a1} { set overlap 1; puts "OVERLAP [lindex $a 0] [lindex $b 0]" }
    }
  }
}
check {$overlap == 0} "data address segments do not overlap"
set gseg [get_bd_addr_segs -of_objects [get_bd_addr_spaces /microblaze_riscv_0/Data] -filter {NAME =~ "*axi_gpio_motor*"}]
check {[get_property OFFSET $gseg] == $gpio_base} "motor GPIO base $gpio_base"
# (변경) 안 A 주소맵 대조: 21장 8줄 + 모터 1줄이 셀 이름 → 베이스·범위로 한 줄씩 같아야 한다
proc data_seg {cell} {
  return [get_bd_addr_segs -of_objects [get_bd_addr_spaces /microblaze_riscv_0/Data] -filter "NAME =~ *_${cell}_*"]
}
set map_a {dlmb_bram_if_cntlr 0x00000000 65536 axi_gpio_0 0x40000000 65536 axi_gpio_btn 0x40010000 65536
  axi_uartlite_0 0x40020000 65536 axi_timer_0 0x40030000 65536 axi_intc_0 0x40040000 65536
  ip06_seg7 0x40060000 65536 axi_gpio_motor 0x40070000 65536 p3_npu_0 0x44A00000 4096}
set addr_ok 1
foreach {cell base rng} $map_a {
  set sg [data_seg $cell]
  if {[llength $sg] != 1} { puts "ADDR_MAP_A $cell MISSING"; set addr_ok 0; continue }
  set ob [get_property OFFSET $sg]; set or [get_property RANGE $sg]
  set ok [expr {$ob == $base && $or == $rng}]
  puts "ADDR_MAP_A $cell [format 0x%08X $ob] $or expect [format 0x%08X $base] $rng [expr {$ok ? "OK" : "MISMATCH"}]"
  if {!$ok} { set addr_ok 0 }
}
if {[llength $segs] != [expr {[llength $map_a] / 3}]} { puts "ADDR_MAP_A extra segments: [llength $segs]"; set addr_ok 0 }
puts [expr {$addr_ok ? "ADDR_MAP_A_PASS" : "ADDR_MAP_A_FAIL"}]
check {$addr_ok} "address map A (21장 8줄 + 모터 1줄)"
# (변경) 안 A 인터럽트 대조: 21장과 같아야 한다(모터는 인터럽트 없음, In4 = 0)
set irq_map_a {0 axi_timer_0/interrupt 1 axi_uartlite_0/interrupt 3 axi_gpio_btn/ip2intc_irpt 12 p3_npu_0/irq_o}
set irq_ok 1
for {set i 0} {$i < 16} {incr i} {
  if {[dict exists $irq_map_a $i]} { set exp [dict get $irq_map_a $i] } else { set exp irq_zero/dout }
  set net [get_bd_nets -quiet -of_objects [get_bd_pins irq_concat/In$i]]
  set drv [expr {$net eq "" ? "" : [get_bd_pins -quiet -of_objects $net -filter {DIR == O}]}]
  set got [string trimleft [lindex $drv 0] /]
  set ok [expr {$got eq $exp}]
  puts "IRQ_MAP_A In$i $got expect $exp [expr {$ok ? "OK" : "MISMATCH"}]"
  if {!$ok} { set irq_ok 0 }
}
if {[get_property CONFIG.NUM_PORTS [get_bd_cells irq_concat]] != 16} { set irq_ok 0 }
puts [expr {$irq_ok ? "IRQ_MAP_A_PASS" : "IRQ_MAP_A_FAIL"}]
check {$irq_ok} "irq map A (21장과 같음)"
check {[get_bd_nets -of_objects [get_bd_pins motor_safety_0/clk]] eq [get_bd_nets -of_objects [get_bd_pins axi_smc/aclk]]} "motor clock = SmartConnect clock"
check {[get_bd_nets -of_objects [get_bd_pins motor_safety_0/resetn]] eq [get_bd_nets -of_objects [get_bd_pins rst_clk_wiz_1_100M/peripheral_aresetn]]} "motor reset = peripheral_aresetn"
foreach port {estop_ok estop_btn fault otemp lim_f lim_r in1 in2 ena trip} {
  check {[llength [get_bd_ports -quiet $port]] == 1} "BD port $port"
}
foreach port {in1 in2 ena} {
  set drv [get_bd_cells -of_objects [get_bd_pins -of_objects [get_bd_nets -of_objects [get_bd_ports $port]]]]
  check {$drv eq "/motor_safety_0"} "BD port $port connects only to motor_safety_0 (not CPU/NPU)"
}
set cmd_src [get_bd_cells -of_objects [get_bd_pins -of_objects [get_bd_nets -of_objects [get_bd_pins motor_safety_0/cmd_i]]]]
check {[lsort $cmd_src] eq [lsort {/axi_gpio_motor /motor_safety_0}]} "motor cmd_i comes only from axi_gpio_motor (CPU register)"

make_wrapper -files $bd_file -top -force
generate_target all $bd_file
create_ip_run [get_files $bd_file]
export_ip_user_files -of_objects $bd_file -no_script -sync -force -quiet
set_property top basys3_soc30_top [current_fileset]
update_compile_order -fileset sources_1
check {[get_property top [current_fileset]] eq "basys3_soc30_top"} "user top basys3_soc30_top"

reset_run synth_1
launch_runs synth_1 -jobs $jobs
wait_on_run synth_1
check {[get_property PROGRESS [get_runs synth_1]] eq "100%"} "synth_1 complete"
launch_runs impl_1 -to_step write_bitstream -jobs $jobs
wait_on_run impl_1
check {[get_property PROGRESS [get_runs impl_1]] eq "100%"} "impl_1 + bitstream complete"
open_run impl_1
report_utilization -file $out/soc_utilization.rpt
report_utilization -hierarchical -hierarchical_depth 3 -file $out/soc_utilization_hier.rpt
report_timing_summary -max_paths 3 -file $out/soc_timing.rpt
report_io -file $out/soc_io.rpt
set wns [get_property SLACK [get_timing_paths -setup -max_paths 1]]
set whs [get_property SLACK [get_timing_paths -hold -max_paths 1]]
puts "TIMING WNS=$wns WHS=$whs"
check {$wns >= 0 && $whs >= 0} "post-route setup/hold met"
foreach {port pin} {JB[0] A14 JB[1] A16 JB[2] B15 JB4 B16 btn[3] T18 btn[0] T17 seg[0] W7 an[0] U2 RsRx B18 led[15] L1 led[12] P3} {
  check {[get_property PACKAGE_PIN [get_ports $port]] eq $pin} "pin $port = $pin"
}
check {[get_property PULLTYPE [get_ports JB4]] eq "PULLDOWN"} "JB4 estop loop PULLDOWN (open = stop)"
set ar [get_cells -hierarchical -filter {NAME =~ "*motor_safety_0*s1_reg*" || NAME =~ "*motor_safety_0*s2_reg*"}]
check {[llength $ar] > 0 && [llength [get_cells $ar -filter {ASYNC_REG == 1}]] == [llength $ar]} "input synchronizers ASYNC_REG ([llength $ar] cells)"
write_hw_platform -fixed -include_bit -force $out/soc30_motor.xsa
foreach f [glob -nocomplain [get_property DIRECTORY [get_runs impl_1]]/*.bit] { file copy -force $f $out/ }
puts "SOC30_BUILD_DONE"
part03/ch30/sw/main.c · c · 원본 · PC 시험 통과 2026-11-19 · 보드 ⚠ 미검증
/* (추가) 30장: MicroBlaze V main. NPU·모터 GPIO 주소는 Vivado Address Editor 값(xparameters.h, 30장 XSA). (변경) 안 A: 모터 GPIO = axi_gpio_motor 0x4007_0000. */
#include "xparameters.h"
#include "motor_app.h"

int main(void)
{
    motor_app_run(XPAR_P3_NPU_0_BASEADDR, XPAR_AXI_GPIO_MOTOR_BASEADDR, 0);   /* 돌아오지 않는다 */
    return 0;
}
part03/ch30/sw/motor_app.h · c · 원본 · PC 시험 통과 2026-11-19 · 보드 ⚠ 미검증
/* (추가) 30장: NPU 추천 + 독립 모터 안전 코어 응용. 규칙 정본은 reference/motor_reference.py·rtl/p3_motor_safety.v. */
#ifndef MOTOR_APP_H
#define MOTOR_APP_H
#include <stdint.h>

#define M_STOP 0
#define M_FWD  1
#define M_REV  2

/* 명령 워드(모터 GPIO axi_gpio_motor 채널 1, 안 A 0x4007_0000): [6:0] duty, [9:8] dir, [12] clear 토글, [15] kick 토글 */
#define MOTOR_CMD(dir, duty, kick, clr) \
    ((uint32_t)(duty) | ((uint32_t)(dir) << 8) | ((uint32_t)(clr) << 12) | ((uint32_t)(kick) << 15))
#define MOTOR_REG_CMD    0x0u   /* AXI GPIO 채널 1 DATA */
#define MOTOR_REG_STATUS 0x8u   /* AXI GPIO 채널 2 DATA */

/* 상태 워드 필드 */
#define MST_DUTY(s)    ((s) & 0x7Fu)
#define MST_DRIVE(s)   (((s) >> 8) & 3u)
#define MST_TRIP(s)    (((s) >> 14) & 1u)

/* 추천: 29장 정책 상태 → (방향, 속도). NORMAL 80 %, WARNING 30 %, FAULT·UNKNOWN STOP */
void motor_recommend(int state, int op_dir, int *dir, int *duty);
/* inject_base: PC 협동 시뮬레이션의 시험 주입 주소(보드 빌드에서는 0 = 'J' 명령 거부) */
int motor_app_run(uintptr_t npu_base, uintptr_t motor_base, uintptr_t inject_base);

#endif
part03/ch30/sw/motor_app.c · c · 원본 · PC 시험 통과 2026-11-19 · 보드 ⚠ 미검증
/* (추가) 30장: NPU 추천 → 독립 모터 안전 코어. 29장 센서 감시(guard·특징·NPU·경보 정책)를 그대로 재사용하고
 * (29장 sensor_app.c를 수정 없이 링크), 정책 상태를 방향·속도 추천으로 바꿔 명령 워드로만 쓴다.
 * CPU는 IN1/IN2/ENA를 직접 구동하지 않는다. E-Stop·고장·과열·워치독·트립·리밋은 RTL 코어가 AI 결과와 무관하게 우선한다.
 * 명령을 쓸 때마다 kick 비트를 토글한다(워치독 갱신). 센서 프레임이 멈추면 kick도 멈추고 코어가 정지·트립한다.
 * 트립 해제('C')는 운전자 명령이며 STOP 명령을 먼저 쓴 뒤 다음 쓰기에서 clear 비트를 토글한다.
 */
#include "app.h"
#include "npu_driver.h"
#include "sensor_app.h"
#include "sensor_io.h"
#include "sensor_model.h"
#include "motor_app.h"

#define FND_ON(m)  ((uint32_t)(m) << 16)
#define LED_LATCH  (1u << 13)
#define LED_ERROR  (1u << 14)
#define LED_ALARM  (1u << 15)

static uint8_t rx_buf[SENSOR_PAYLOAD];
static int8_t x_buf[NPU_N_IN];
static policy_t pol;
static uintptr_t mbase, ibase;
static int kick, clr, op_dir = M_FWD, rec_dir, rec_duty, model_ok, bad_streak;

void motor_recommend(int state, int op, int *dir, int *duty)
{
    if (state == ST_NORMAL || state == ST_WARNING) {
        *dir = op;
        *duty = state == ST_NORMAL ? 80 : 30;
    } else {
        *dir = M_STOP;
        *duty = 0;
    }
}

static void motor_write(void)
{
    kick ^= 1;
    npu_io_write(mbase + MOTOR_REG_CMD, MOTOR_CMD(rec_dir, rec_duty, kick, clr));
}

static void command_from_policy(void)
{
    motor_recommend(pol.state, op_dir, &rec_dir, &rec_duty);
    motor_write();
}

static uint16_t crc16(const uint8_t *p, uint32_t n, uint16_t crc)
{
    uint32_t i;
    int b;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        crc ^= (uint16_t)p[i] << 8;
        for (b = 0; b < 8; b++)
            crc = (crc & 0x8000u) ? (uint16_t)((crc << 1) ^ 0x1021u) : (uint16_t)(crc << 1);
    }
    return crc;
}

/* FND: [3] 정책 상태, [2] 실제 구동 방향(점 = 모터 트립), [1:0] 실제 duty(16진). LED는 CPU 몫만(9..12는 Top이 핀 표시) */
static void show(uint32_t st, int err)
{
    uint32_t fnd = ((uint32_t)pol.state << 12) | (MST_DRIVE(st) << 8) | MST_DUTY(st) | FND_ON(0xF)
                   | (MST_TRIP(st) ? (1u << 22) : 0);
    uint32_t led = (1u << pol.state) | (pol.state != ST_NORMAL ? LED_ALARM : 0) | (pol.latch ? LED_LATCH : 0)
                   | (err ? LED_ERROR : 0);
    sio_display(fnd, led);
    APP_PRINTF("DISP fnd=%08x led=%04x\r\n", (unsigned)fnd, (unsigned)led);
    sio_flush();
}

static void report(const char *src, int seq, const char *guard, int npu, int ok, int junk)
{
    uint32_t st = npu_io_read(mbase + MOTOR_REG_STATUS);
    APP_PRINTF("MOTOR src=%s seq=%d guard=%s npu=%d state=%d latch=%d ok=%d op_dir=%d rec_dir=%d rec_duty=%d st=%08x junk=%d\r\n",
               src, seq, guard, npu, pol.state, pol.latch, ok, op_dir, rec_dir, rec_duty, (unsigned)st, junk);
    show(st, 0);
}

static void proto_error(int seq, int code, const char *name, int detail, int junk)
{
    bad_streak++;
    APP_PRINTF("ERROR seq=%d code=%d name=%s detail=%d junk=%d bad=%d\r\n", seq, code, name, detail, junk, bad_streak);
    if (bad_streak >= LINK_BAD_N) {                    /* 연속 프로토콜 오류 = 링크 이상 → UNKNOWN → STOP 추천 */
        policy_cand(&pol, ST_UNKNOWN);
        command_from_policy();
    }
    show(npu_io_read(mbase + MOTOR_REG_STATUS), 1);
}

static void sensor_frame(npu_t *npu, int junk)
{
    sensor_win_t s;
    int64_t ns[NPU_N_CLASS], cs[NPU_N_CLASS];
    int seq = rx_buf[0] | (rx_buf[1] << 8);
    uint32_t mid = rx_buf[2] | ((uint32_t)rx_buf[3] << 8) | ((uint32_t)rx_buf[4] << 16) | ((uint32_t)rx_buf[5] << 24);
    const uint8_t *p = rx_buf + 6;
    int i, c, rc, fmask, umask, ncls = -1, ccls, same = 1;
    int8_t f[SENSOR_N_FEAT];
    if (mid != SENSOR_MODEL_ID || !model_ok) {
        proto_error(seq, SERR_MODEL, "MODEL", model_ok, junk);
        return;
    }
    bad_streak = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < 3; i++, p += 2)
        s.age[i] = (uint16_t)(p[0] | (p[1] << 8));
    for (i = 0; i < SENSOR_WIN; i++, p += 6) {
        s.t[i] = (int16_t)(uint16_t)(p[0] | (p[1] << 8));
        s.v[i] = (uint16_t)(p[2] | (p[3] << 8));
        s.d[i] = (uint16_t)(p[4] | (p[5] << 8));
    }
    sensor_guard(&s, &fmask, &umask);                 /* 29장: 규칙 먼저 */
    if (fmask || umask) {
        policy_cand(&pol, fmask ? ST_FAULT : ST_UNKNOWN);
        command_from_policy();
        report("FRAME", seq, fmask ? "FAULT" : "UNKNOWN", -1, -1, junk);
        return;
    }
    sensor_features(&s, f);
    for (i = 0; i < SENSOR_N_FEAT; i++)
        x_buf[i] = f[i];
    rc = npu_infer(npu, x_buf, model_w, ns, &ncls);
    if (rc == NPU_OK) {
        npu_clear_done(npu);
        ccls = cpu_infer(x_buf, model_w, model_b, cs);
        for (c = 0; c < NPU_N_CLASS; c++)
            same &= ns[c] == cs[c];
        same &= ncls == ccls && ncls >= 0 && ncls < SENSOR_N_CLS;
    }
    if (rc != NPU_OK || !same) {                       /* NPU 오류·불일치 = UNKNOWN → STOP 추천 */
        APP_PRINTF("ERROR seq=%d code=%d name=%s detail=%d junk=%d bad=%d\r\n", seq,
                   rc != NPU_OK ? SERR_NPU : SERR_MISMATCH, rc != NPU_OK ? "NPU" : "MISMATCH", rc, junk, bad_streak);
        policy_cand(&pol, ST_UNKNOWN);
        command_from_policy();
        report("NPU", seq, "OK", ncls, -1, junk);
        return;
    }
    policy_cand(&pol, ncls);
    command_from_policy();
    report("FRAME", seq, "OK", ncls, -1, junk);
}

/* 'J' 시험 주입(PC 협동 시뮬레이션 전용): op 1 = 입력 비트 {lim_r, lim_f, otemp, fault, estop}, op 2 = N클록 진행 */
static void inject(int junk)
{
    int op = rx_buf[0];
    uint32_t v = rx_buf[1] | ((uint32_t)rx_buf[2] << 8) | ((uint32_t)rx_buf[3] << 16) | ((uint32_t)rx_buf[4] << 24);
    if (!ibase || (op != 1 && op != 2)) {
        proto_error(-1, SERR_CMD, "CMD", 'J', junk);
        return;
    }
    npu_io_write(ibase + (op == 1 ? 0x0u : 0x4u), v);
    report(op == 1 ? "INJECT" : "ADVANCE", -1, "-", -1, -1, junk);
}

static int handle(npu_t *npu)
{
    int c, prev = -1, junk = 0, cmd;
    uint32_t i, len;
    uint16_t crc;
    uint8_t b;
    for (;;) {
        c = sio_getc(SIO_WAIT_LINK);
        if (c == SIO_EOF)
            return -1;
        if (c == SIO_TIMEOUT) {                        /* 센서 프레임 없음 → UNKNOWN → STOP 추천 */
            policy_cand(&pol, ST_UNKNOWN);
            command_from_policy();
            report("LINK_TIMEOUT", -1, "LINK", -1, -1, junk);
            return 0;
        }
        if (prev == 'P' && c == '3')
            break;
        if (prev >= 0)
            junk++;
        prev = c;
    }
    cmd = sio_getc(SIO_IN_FRAME);
    if (cmd < 0) {
        proto_error(-1, SERR_TIMEOUT, "TIMEOUT", 0, junk);
        return cmd == SIO_EOF ? -1 : 0;
    }
    if (cmd == 'S')
        len = SENSOR_PAYLOAD;
    else if (cmd == 'D')
        len = 1;
    else if (cmd == 'J')
        len = 5;
    else if (cmd == 'I' || cmd == 'A' || cmd == 'C' || cmd == 'Q')
        len = 0;
    else {
        while ((c = sio_getc(SIO_IN_FRAME)) >= 0)
            ;
        proto_error(-1, SERR_CMD, "CMD", cmd, junk);
        return c == SIO_EOF ? -1 : 0;
    }
    b = (uint8_t)cmd;
    crc = crc16(&b, 1, 0xFFFFu);
    for (i = 0; i < len + 2; i++) {
        c = sio_getc(SIO_IN_FRAME);
        if (c < 0) {
            proto_error(-1, SERR_TIMEOUT, "TIMEOUT", (int)i, junk);
            return c == SIO_EOF ? -1 : 0;
        }
        if (i < len)
            rx_buf[i] = (uint8_t)c;
        b = (uint8_t)c;
        crc = crc16(&b, 1, crc);
    }
    if (crc != 0) {
        proto_error(-1, SERR_CRC, "CRC", (int)len, junk);
        return 0;
    }
    switch (cmd) {
    case 'I':
        APP_PRINTF("P3_MOTOR READY model=%08x model_ok=%d junk=%d\r\n", (unsigned)SENSOR_MODEL_ID,
                   sensor_model_crc32() == SENSOR_MODEL_ID, junk);
        sio_flush();
        break;
    case 'S':
        sensor_frame(npu, junk);
        break;
    case 'D':                                          /* 운전자 방향(1 FWD, 2 REV). 속도는 계속 정책 상태가 정한다 */
        if (rx_buf[0] != M_FWD && rx_buf[0] != M_REV) {
            proto_error(-1, SERR_CMD, "DIR", rx_buf[0], junk);
            break;
        }
        op_dir = rx_buf[0];
        command_from_policy();
        report("DIR", -1, "-", -1, -1, junk);
        break;
    case 'A':                                          /* 29장 경보 래치 ACK(모터 트립 해제가 아니다) */
        c = policy_ack(&pol);
        report("ACK", -1, "-", -1, c, junk);
        break;
    case 'C':                                          /* 모터 트립 해제: STOP 쓰기 → 다음 쓰기에서 clear 토글 */
        rec_dir = M_STOP;
        rec_duty = 0;
        motor_write();
        clr ^= 1;
        npu_io_write(mbase + MOTOR_REG_CMD, MOTOR_CMD(rec_dir, rec_duty, kick, clr));
        report("CLEAR", -1, "-", -1, -1, junk);
        break;
    case 'Q':                                          /* 상태 조회: 명령을 쓰지 않는다(워치독 갱신 없음) */
        report("QUERY", -1, "-", -1, -1, junk);
        break;
    default:
        inject(junk);
        break;
    }
    return 0;
}

int motor_app_run(uintptr_t npu_base, uintptr_t motor_base, uintptr_t inject_base)
{
    npu_t npu;
    int n = 0, rc;
    uint32_t mcrc = sensor_model_crc32();
    mbase = motor_base;
    ibase = inject_base;
    policy_reset(&pol);                                /* 29장: 부팅 UNKNOWN → 추천 STOP */
    motor_recommend(pol.state, op_dir, &rec_dir, &rec_duty);
    npu_io_write(mbase + MOTOR_REG_CMD, MOTOR_CMD(rec_dir, rec_duty, kick, clr));   /* kick 토글 없음: 명령 아님 */
    npu_init(&npu, npu_base);
    rc = npu_set_bias(&npu, model_b);
    model_ok = mcrc == SENSOR_MODEL_ID && rc == NPU_OK;
    APP_PRINTF("P3_MOTOR READY model=%08x model_ok=%d bias_rc=%d\r\n", (unsigned)SENSOR_MODEL_ID, model_ok, rc);
    report("BOOT", -1, "-", -1, -1, 0);
    while (handle(&npu) == 0)
        n++;
    return n;
}
part03/ch30/host/p3_motor_host.py · python · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""30장 PC 호스트: 가상 센서 창 → 보드(또는 협동 시뮬레이션) → 정책 상태·추천·모터 상태 워드 판정 → CSV.

  --cosim "<명령>"  PC 빌드 펌웨어 + Icarus(20장 NPU + 30장 모터 안전 코어). 'J' 주입으로 E-Stop·고장·과열·리밋·
                    클록 진행(워치독)을 넣는다. 코어 파라미터는 motor_reference.COSIM.
  --port <포트>     Basys3(30장 Bitstream + ELF). 'J'는 거부된다 → 자동 단계만 하고 버튼·스위치 단계는 점검표(B18~)로.
판정: 정책(state·latch·npu·guard)은 29장 Firmware 모델과 같아야 하고, 추천(rec_dir·rec_duty)은 motor_recommend 규칙,
      모터 상태 워드(st)는 단계별 술어(예: E-Stop 중 in1=0·ESTOP 래치, AI가 NORMAL이어도 구동 없음)를 만족해야 한다.
      핀 단위 안전 불변식은 협동 시뮬레이션 브리지 TB가 모든 클록에서 따로 센다(SAFETY_SUMMARY).
"""
import argparse
import csv
import struct
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]))
from part03.ch29.host.p3_sensor_host import ProcTransport, SerialTransport, pick  # noqa: E402
from part03.ch29.reference.sensor_reference import (  # noqa: E402
    FAULT, NORMAL, T_MISS, TEST, UNKNOWN, WARNING, Firmware, dataset, frame, load_qmodel, model_id, parse_kv,
    sensor_frame,
)
from part03.ch30.reference.motor_reference import COSIM, FWD, REV, STOP  # noqa: E402

DUTY = {NORMAL: 80, WARNING: 30}
I_ESTOP, I_FAULT, I_OTEMP, I_LIMF, I_LIMR = 1, 2, 4, 8, 16
L_ESTOP, L_FAULT, L_OTEMP, L_WD, L_RESET = 1, 2, 4, 8, 16


def recommend(state, op_dir):
    return (op_dir, DUTY[state]) if state in DUTY else (STOP, 0)


def fields(st):
    return dict(duty=st & 0x7F, err=st >> 7 & 1, drive=st >> 8 & 3, req=st >> 10 & 3, gate=st >> 12 & 1,
                dead=st >> 13 & 1, trip=st >> 14 & 1, wd=st >> 15 & 1, latched=st >> 16 & 31, inputs=st >> 21 & 31,
                in1=st >> 26 & 1, in2=st >> 27 & 1, ena=st >> 28 & 1)


class Host:
    def __init__(self, tr, timeout, log, writer, target):
        self.tr, self.timeout, self.log, self.w, self.target = tr, timeout, log, writer, target
        self.fw = Firmware(load_qmodel())
        self.mid = self.fw.mid
        self.op_dir, self.n, self.passed, self.fails, self.step = FWD, 0, 0, [], 0

    def block(self):
        lines = []
        while True:
            line = self.tr.readline(self.timeout)
            if line is None:
                return lines
            self.log.write(line + "\n")
            lines.append(line)
            if line.startswith("DISP") or (line.startswith("P3_MOTOR") and len(lines) == 1):
                return lines

    def run(self, name, data, kind="MOTOR", src=None, policy=None, pred=None, why=""):
        """policy: 정책 기대(29장 Firmware 결과 dict) 또는 None. pred(f): 모터 상태 술어 → 실패 설명 목록."""
        if data:
            self.tr.write(data)
        lines = self.block()
        errs = []
        main = next((ln for ln in lines if ln.startswith(kind)), None)
        kv = parse_kv(main or "")
        f = fields(int(kv["st"], 16)) if "st" in kv else {}
        if main is None:
            errs.append(f"no {kind}: {lines}")
        elif kind == "MOTOR":
            if src and kv.get("src") != src:
                errs.append(f"src={kv.get('src')}")
            fw = self.fw
            if policy is not None:
                for k in ("state", "latch"):
                    if kv.get(k) != str(policy[k]):
                        errs.append(f"{k}={kv.get(k)}/{policy[k]}")
                if "guard" in policy and kv.get("guard") != policy["guard"]:
                    errs.append(f"guard={kv.get('guard')}/{policy['guard']}")
                if policy.get("guard") == "OK" and kv.get("npu") != str(policy["npu"]):
                    errs.append(f"npu={kv.get('npu')}/{policy['npu']}")
            if kv.get("state") != str(fw.pol.state):
                errs.append(f"policy state={kv.get('state')}/{fw.pol.state}")
            rd, ru = recommend(fw.pol.state, self.op_dir)
            if src not in ("QUERY", "INJECT", "ADVANCE", "ACK", "BOOT", "CLEAR") and \
                    (kv.get("rec_dir"), kv.get("rec_duty")) != (str(rd), str(ru)):
                errs.append(f"rec={kv.get('rec_dir')},{kv.get('rec_duty')}/{rd},{ru}")
            if pred:
                errs += pred(f)
            disp = parse_kv(lines[-1]) if lines and lines[-1].startswith("DISP") else None
            fnd = (fw.pol.state << 12 | f.get("drive", 0) << 8 | f.get("duty", 0) | 0xF << 16
                   | (f.get("trip", 0) << 22))
            led = (1 << fw.pol.state) | (1 << 15 if fw.pol.state != NORMAL else 0) | (1 << 13 if fw.pol.latch else 0)
            if not disp or (int(disp["fnd"], 16), int(disp["led"], 16)) != (fnd, led):
                errs.append(f"disp={disp}/{fnd:08x},{led:04x}")
        elif kind == "ERROR" and pred:
            errs += pred(kv)
        ok = not errs
        self.step += 1
        self.n += 1
        self.passed += ok
        if not ok:
            self.fails.append((self.step, name, errs))
        self.w.writerow(dict(target=self.target, step=self.step, name=name, why=why, src=kv.get("src", kind),
                             state=kv.get("state", ""), rec_dir=kv.get("rec_dir", ""), rec_duty=kv.get("rec_duty", ""),
                             st=kv.get("st", ""), drive=f.get("drive", ""), duty=f.get("duty", ""),
                             trip=f.get("trip", ""), latched=f.get("latched", ""), ok=int(ok), detail=";".join(errs)))
        print(f"STEP {self.step:3d} {name:22s} {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} {';'.join(errs) or (main or '')[:150]}")
        return f

    # ---- 동작 단위
    def sensor(self, name, s, seq, pred=None, why=""):
        fw = self.fw
        e = fw.sensor(seq, self.mid, s)
        return self.run(name, sensor_frame(seq, self.mid, s), src="FRAME", policy=e, pred=pred, why=why)

    def inject(self, name, mask, pred=None):
        return self.run(name, frame(b"J", struct.pack("<BI", 1, mask)), src="INJECT", pred=pred)

    def advance(self, name, n, pred=None):
        return self.run(name, frame(b"J", struct.pack("<BI", 2, n)), src="ADVANCE", pred=pred)

    def clear(self, name, pred=None):
        return self.run(name, frame(b"C"), src="CLEAR", pred=pred)

    def direction(self, name, d, pred=None):
        self.op_dir = d
        return self.run(name, frame(b"D", bytes([d])), src="DIR", pred=pred)

    def ack(self, name, pred=None):
        self.fw.pol.ack()
        return self.run(name, frame(b"A"), src="ACK", pred=pred)


def P(**want):
    """상태 워드 필드 = 값 술어."""
    def pred(f):
        return [f"{k}={f.get(k)}/{v}" for k, v in want.items() if f.get(k) != v]
    return pred


def has_latch(bit, present=True):
    def pred(f):
        return [] if bool(f["latched"] & bit) == present else [f"latched={f['latched']:05b} bit {bit} {present}"]
    return pred


def all_of(*ps):
    return lambda f: [e for p in ps for e in p(f)]


def cosim_suite(h, picks):
    N, W, F = (iter(x) for x in picks)
    seq = iter(range(300, 2000))
    nxt = {NORMAL: N, WARNING: W, FAULT: F}
    run_up = COSIM["DEAD_CYC"] + 80 * COSIM["RAMP_CYC"] + 2 * COSIM["PWM_PERIOD"]

    def s(c, name, pred=None, why=""):
        return h.sensor(name, next(nxt[c]), next(seq), pred, why)

    stopped = P(in1=0, in2=0, ena=0)
    for i in range(3):                                # 부팅 트립(RESET): NPU가 NORMAL이어도 움직이지 않는다
        s(NORMAL, f"boot_normal_{i}", all_of(stopped, has_latch(L_RESET)), "자동 재가동 금지")
    h.advance("boot_wait", run_up, all_of(stopped, P(trip=1)))
    h.clear("clear_reset", P(trip=0, latched=0, req=STOP))
    s(NORMAL, "normal_run", P(req=FWD, trip=0))
    h.advance("ramp_to_80", run_up, P(drive=FWD, duty=80, in1=1, in2=0, gate=1))
    s(WARNING, "warning_slow", P(req=FWD, duty=30, drive=FWD), "감속은 즉시")
    h.inject("estop_on", I_ESTOP, all_of(stopped, P(gate=0, drive=STOP), has_latch(L_ESTOP)))
    for i in range(3):                                # AI 결과가 NORMAL로 내려와도 E-Stop이 우선
        s(NORMAL, f"estop_ai_normal_{i}", all_of(stopped, has_latch(L_ESTOP)), "AI는 E-Stop을 넘을 수 없다")
    h.advance("estop_hold", run_up, stopped)
    h.inject("estop_off", 0, all_of(stopped, P(trip=1), has_latch(L_ESTOP)))
    s(NORMAL, "after_release_still_trip", all_of(stopped, P(trip=1)), "래치: 해제 명령 전까지 정지")
    h.clear("clear_estop", P(trip=0, latched=0))
    s(NORMAL, "resume", P(req=FWD))
    h.advance("resume_run", run_up, P(drive=FWD, duty=80, in1=1))
    h.inject("limit_fwd", I_LIMF, all_of(stopped, P(trip=0, drive=STOP)))   # 래치 없음, 진행 방향만 차단
    h.direction("reverse_off_limit", REV, P(req=REV))
    h.advance("reverse_run", run_up, P(drive=REV, in2=1, in1=0, trip=0))
    h.inject("limit_fwd_off", 0, P(drive=REV, in2=1))
    h.direction("forward_again", FWD, all_of(stopped, P(req=FWD)))
    h.advance("dead_time_half", COSIM["DEAD_CYC"] // 2, all_of(stopped, P(dead=1)), )
    h.advance("dead_time_done", run_up, P(drive=FWD, in1=1))
    s(FAULT, "npu_fault_stop", all_of(stopped, P(req=STOP, trip=0)), "AI FAULT → STOP 추천")
    for i in range(3):
        s(NORMAL, f"policy_latched_{i}", all_of(stopped, P(req=STOP)), "29장 FAULT 래치")
    h.ack("policy_ack")
    for i in range(3):
        s(NORMAL, f"after_ack_{i}", None)
    h.advance("run_after_ack", run_up, P(drive=FWD, duty=80, in1=1))
    base = picks[0][-1]
    h.sensor("temp_missing", dict(base, t=[T_MISS] + base["t"][1:]), next(seq), all_of(stopped, P(req=STOP)), "guard")
    for i in range(3):
        s(NORMAL, f"recover_{i}", None)
    h.advance("run_again", run_up, P(drive=FWD, in1=1))
    for name, bit, lb in (("fault", I_FAULT, L_FAULT), ("otemp", I_OTEMP, L_OTEMP)):
        h.inject(f"{name}_on", bit, all_of(stopped, has_latch(lb)))
        h.inject(f"{name}_off", 0, all_of(stopped, P(trip=1)))
        h.clear(f"clear_{name}", P(trip=0))
        s(NORMAL, f"{name}_resume", P(req=FWD))
        h.advance(f"{name}_run", run_up, P(drive=FWD, in1=1))
    h.run("query_no_kick", frame(b"Q"), src="QUERY", pred=P(drive=FWD))
    h.advance("watchdog_expire", COSIM["WD_CYC"] + 10, all_of(stopped, P(wd=1), has_latch(L_WD)), )
    s(NORMAL, "kick_after_wd", all_of(stopped, P(wd=0, trip=1)), "kick으로 만료는 풀려도 래치 유지")
    h.clear("clear_wd", P(trip=0, latched=0))
    s(NORMAL, "final_run", P(req=FWD))
    h.advance("final_ramp", run_up, P(drive=FWD, duty=80, in1=1))
    bad = bytearray(sensor_frame(999, h.mid, base))
    bad[20] ^= 1
    h.fw.proto_error("CRC")
    h.run("crc_error", bytes(bad), kind="ERROR", pred=lambda kv: [] if kv.get("code") == "1" else ["code"])
    h.fw.proto_error("CMD")
    h.run("bad_direction", frame(b"D", b"\x03"), kind="ERROR", pred=lambda kv: [] if kv.get("name") == "DIR" else ["name"])
    h.fw.proto_error("CMD")                           # 연속 3회 → UNKNOWN → STOP 추천
    h.run("bad_inject_op", frame(b"J", struct.pack("<BI", 9, 0)), kind="ERROR",
          pred=lambda kv: [] if kv.get("code") == "3" else ["code"])
    h.run("after_link_bad", frame(b"Q"), src="QUERY", pred=P(req=STOP))


def board_suite(h, picks):
    """주입 없이: 부팅 트립 → 해제 → NORMAL 운전(램프) → WARNING 감속 → FAULT 추천 정지 → 'J' 거부."""
    N, W, F = (iter(x) for x in picks)
    seq = iter(range(300, 2000))
    stopped = P(in1=0, in2=0, ena=0)
    h.sensor("boot_normal", next(N), next(seq), all_of(stopped, P(trip=1)))
    h.clear("clear_reset", P(trip=0))
    for i in range(10):                               # 약 0.2 s 동안 프레임 반복(DEAD 100 ms + 램프 80 ms)
        f = h.sensor(f"run_{i}", next(N), next(seq), P(req=FWD, trip=0))
        time.sleep(0.02)
    h.sensor("run_check", next(N), next(seq), P(drive=FWD, duty=80))
    h.sensor("warning", next(W), next(seq), P(duty=30))
    h.sensor("npu_fault", next(F), next(seq), all_of(stopped, P(req=STOP)))
    h.run("inject_rejected", frame(b"J", struct.pack("<BI", 1, 1)), kind="ERROR")
    h.fw.proto_error("CMD")
    return f


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    g = p.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    g.add_argument("--port")
    g.add_argument("--cosim")
    p.add_argument("--baud", type=int, default=115200)
    p.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=60.0)
    p.add_argument("--csv", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--log", type=Path, required=True)
    a = p.parse_args()
    qm = load_qmodel()
    ds = dataset(*TEST)
    picks = [pick(ds, qm, c, 40) for c in (NORMAL, WARNING, FAULT)]   # 연결 전에 계산(링크 감시 회피)
    tr = SerialTransport(a.port, a.baud) if a.port else ProcTransport(a.cosim)
    target = "fpga" if a.port else "rtl_cosim"
    with a.log.open("w") as log, a.csv.open("w", newline="") as fcsv:
        w = csv.DictWriter(fcsv, fieldnames=["target", "step", "name", "why", "src", "state", "rec_dir", "rec_duty",
                                             "st", "drive", "duty", "trip", "latched", "ok", "detail"])
        w.writeheader()
        h = Host(tr, a.timeout, log, w, target)
        if a.port:
            print("보드의 btnC(Reset)를 누르세요: 부팅 줄(P3_MOTOR READY)을 기다립니다")
            tr.s.reset_input_buffer()
            line = None
            while line is None or not line.startswith("P3_MOTOR READY"):
                line = tr.readline(120)
                if line is None:
                    print("MOTOR_HOST_FAIL no boot line")
                    return 1
        else:
            line = tr.readline(a.timeout)
        log.write(f"{line}\n")
        kv = parse_kv(line or "")
        boot_ok = bool(line) and int(kv.get("model", "0"), 16) == h.mid and kv.get("model_ok") == "1"
        h.n += 1
        h.passed += boot_ok
        print(f"BOOT {'PASS' if boot_ok else 'FAIL'} :: {line}")
        h.run("boot_status", b"", src="BOOT", pred=all_of(P(in1=0, in2=0, ena=0, trip=1, req=STOP),
                                                           has_latch(L_RESET)))
        main_line = h.run("identify", frame(b"I"), kind="P3_MOTOR")
        del main_line
        if a.port:
            board_suite(h, picks)
        else:
            cosim_suite(h, picks)
    rc = tr.close()
    print(f"MOTOR_HOST_SUMMARY target={target} checks={h.passed}/{h.n} proc_rc={rc}")
    for step, name, errs in h.fails[:10]:
        print(f"FAIL_DETAIL step={step} {name} {';'.join(errs)}")
    ok = h.passed == h.n and rc == 0
    print("MOTOR_HOST_PASS" if ok else "MOTOR_HOST_FAIL")
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

part03/ch30/tb/tb_p3_motor_safety.sv · systemverilog · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
// (추가) 30장: p3_motor_safety 클록 단위 벡터 시험(UNIT 파라미터). Python motor_reference.unit_vectors가 만든
//   입력(rst cmd estop fault otemp lim_f lim_r)과 기대 출력(in1 in2 ena status 32비트)을 클록마다 대조한다.
//   Python 쪽에서 같은 출력이 안전 불변식(정지 조건 중 출력 0, IN1·IN2 동시 금지, 반전 데드타임)을 지키는지도 확인한다.
`timescale 1ns/1ps
module tb_p3_motor_safety;
    reg clk = 0, rst = 1, estop = 0, fault = 0, otemp = 0, lim_f = 0, lim_r = 0;
    reg [31:0] cmd = 0;
    wire in1, in2, ena;
    wire [31:0] status;
    always #5 clk = ~clk;

    p3_motor_safety #(.PWM_PERIOD(100), .DEAD_CYC(9), .WD_CYC(70), .RAMP_CYC(3)) dut(
        .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .cmd(cmd), .estop(estop), .fault(fault), .otemp(otemp), .lim_f(lim_f), .lim_r(lim_r),
        .in1(in1), .in2(in2), .ena(ena), .status(status));

    reg [8*256-1:0] vpath;
    integer f, r, n = 0, errors = 0, e_rst, e_es, e_fa, e_ot, e_lf, e_lr, x1, x2, xe, n_ena = 0, n_trip = 0;
    reg [31:0] e_cmd, xs;
    initial begin
        if (!$value$plusargs("VEC=%s", vpath)) $fatal(1, "+VEC required");
        f = $fopen(vpath, "r");
        if (f == 0) $fatal(1, "cannot open vectors");
        @(negedge clk);
        while (!$feof(f)) begin
            r = $fscanf(f, "%d %h %d %d %d %d %d %d %d %d %h\n", e_rst, e_cmd, e_es, e_fa, e_ot, e_lf, e_lr, x1, x2, xe, xs);
            if (r == 11) begin
                rst = e_rst; cmd = e_cmd; estop = e_es; fault = e_fa; otemp = e_ot; lim_f = e_lf; lim_r = e_lr;
                #1;
                if (!e_rst && (in1 !== x1[0] || in2 !== x2[0] || ena !== xe[0] || status !== xs)) begin
                    errors = errors + 1;
                    if (errors <= 10)
                        $display("MISMATCH row=%0d cmd=%h got %0d%0d%0d %h exp %0d%0d%0d %h", n, cmd, in1, in2, ena, status,
                                 x1, x2, xe, xs);
                end
                if (ena) n_ena = n_ena + 1;
                if (status[14]) n_trip = n_trip + 1;
                @(posedge clk);
                n = n + 1;
                @(negedge clk);
            end
        end
        if (errors == 0 && n > 1000 && n_ena > 0)
            $display("RTL_SIM_PASS MOTOR_SAFETY cycles=%0d ena_cycles=%0d trip_cycles=%0d", n, n_ena, n_trip);
        else
            $display("RTL_SIM_FAIL MOTOR_SAFETY cycles=%0d errors=%0d", n, errors);
        $finish;
    end
endmodule
part03/ch30/tb/tb_p3_motor_bridge.sv · systemverilog · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
// (추가) 30장 협동 시뮬레이션 브리지. 22장 tb_p3_npu_bridge와 같은 FIFO 규약에 모터 안전 코어를 더했다.
//   주소 0x00..0x3F → 20장 AXI NPU(BFM), 0x100 쓰기 → 명령 워드(AXI GPIO 2 채널 1과 같은 값), 0x108 읽기 → 상태 워드
//   (채널 2), 0x200 쓰기 → 시험 주입: 입력 {lim_r, lim_f, otemp, fault, estop}, 0x204 쓰기 → N클록 진행.
//   주입 주소는 시험 장치(보드의 버튼·스위치·외부 회로에 해당)이며 펌웨어의 PC 빌드만 쓴다.
//   모든 클록에서 핀(IN1·IN2·ENA)의 안전 불변식을 감시하고 끝에 SAFETY_SUMMARY를 낸다.
//   GPIO 쓰기·읽기에는 각각 GPIO_LAT클록 버스 지연을 둔다(실제 AXI GPIO 지연과 비슷하게, 정확히 같지는 않다).
`timescale 1ns/1ps
module tb_p3_motor_bridge;
    reg clk = 0, resetn = 0;
    always #5 clk = ~clk;
    reg [5:0] awaddr = 0, araddr = 0;
    reg awvalid = 0, wvalid = 0, bready = 0, arvalid = 0, rready = 0;
    reg [31:0] wdata = 0;
    reg [3:0] wstrb = 0;
    wire awready, wready, bvalid, arready, rvalid, irq;
    wire [1:0] bresp, rresp;
    wire [31:0] rdata;

    p3_npu_axi_lite npu(
        .s_axi_aclk(clk), .s_axi_aresetn(resetn),
        .s_axi_awaddr(awaddr), .s_axi_awprot(3'd0), .s_axi_awvalid(awvalid), .s_axi_awready(awready),
        .s_axi_wdata(wdata), .s_axi_wstrb(wstrb), .s_axi_wvalid(wvalid), .s_axi_wready(wready),
        .s_axi_bresp(bresp), .s_axi_bvalid(bvalid), .s_axi_bready(bready),
        .s_axi_araddr(araddr), .s_axi_arprot(3'd0), .s_axi_arvalid(arvalid), .s_axi_arready(arready),
        .s_axi_rdata(rdata), .s_axi_rresp(rresp), .s_axi_rvalid(rvalid), .s_axi_rready(rready),
        .irq_o(irq));

    `include "axi_lite_bfm.svh"

    localparam integer DEAD = 3000, GPIO_LAT = 4;
    reg [31:0] mcmd = 0;
    reg [4:0] inj = 0;                       // {lim_r, lim_f, otemp, fault, estop}
    wire in1, in2, ena;
    wire [31:0] mst;
    p3_motor_safety #(.PWM_PERIOD(200), .DEAD_CYC(DEAD), .WD_CYC(400000), .RAMP_CYC(40)) motor(
        .clk(clk), .rst(!resetn), .cmd(mcmd), .estop(inj[0]), .fault(inj[1]), .otemp(inj[2]),
        .lim_f(inj[3]), .lim_r(inj[4]), .in1(in1), .in2(in2), .ena(ena), .status(mst));

    // ---- 핀 안전 불변식(클록마다)
    integer viol = 0, n_cyc = 0, n_ena = 0, n_in1 = 0, n_in2 = 0, off_run = 1000000000, reversals = 0, min_rev_gap = 1000000000;
    integer last_dir = 0, cur_dir;
    always @(posedge clk) if (resetn) begin
        n_cyc = n_cyc + 1;
        if ((in1 || in2 || ena) && (inj[0] || inj[1] || inj[2] || mst[14] || mst[15])) begin
            viol = viol + 1; if (viol <= 5) $display("SAFETY_VIOLATION t=%0t output during stop condition st=%h", $time, mst);
        end
        if (in1 && in2) begin viol = viol + 1; if (viol <= 5) $display("SAFETY_VIOLATION t=%0t in1&in2", $time); end
        if ((in1 && inj[3]) || (in2 && inj[4])) begin viol = viol + 1; if (viol <= 5) $display("SAFETY_VIOLATION t=%0t limit", $time); end
        if (ena && !(in1 || in2)) begin viol = viol + 1; if (viol <= 5) $display("SAFETY_VIOLATION t=%0t ena alone", $time); end
        cur_dir = in1 ? 1 : in2 ? 2 : 0;
        if (cur_dir != 0) begin
            if (last_dir != 0 && cur_dir != last_dir) begin
                reversals = reversals + 1;
                if (off_run < min_rev_gap) min_rev_gap = off_run;
                if (off_run < DEAD) begin viol = viol + 1; $display("SAFETY_VIOLATION t=%0t reversal gap %0d", $time, off_run); end
            end
            last_dir = cur_dir; off_run = 0;
        end else off_run = off_run + 1;
        if (ena) n_ena = n_ena + 1;
        if (in1) n_in1 = n_in1 + 1;
        if (in2) n_in2 = n_in2 + 1;
    end

    reg [8*256-1:0] cpath, rpath;
    reg [8*8-1:0] op;
    reg [31:0] a, d;
    reg [1:0] resp;
    integer cf, rf, rc, n_wr = 0, n_rd = 0, k;
    initial begin
        if (!$value$plusargs("CMD=%s", cpath)) $fatal(1, "+CMD required");
        if (!$value$plusargs("RSP=%s", rpath)) $fatal(1, "+RSP required");
        repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
        resetn = 1;
        cf = $fopen(cpath, "r");
        rf = $fopen(rpath, "w");
        forever begin
            rc = $fscanf(cf, "%s", op);
            if (rc != 1) $fatal(1, "command stream closed without Q");
            if (op == "Q") begin
                $display("SAFETY_SUMMARY cycles=%0d violations=%0d ena_cycles=%0d in1_cycles=%0d in2_cycles=%0d reversals=%0d min_reversal_gap=%0d dead=%0d",
                         n_cyc, viol, n_ena, n_in1, n_in2, reversals, min_rev_gap, DEAD);
                if (viol == 0 && n_ena > 0 && reversals > 0)
                    $display("RTL_SIM_PASS MOTOR_BRIDGE axi_writes=%0d axi_reads=%0d", n_wr, n_rd);
                else
                    $display("RTL_SIM_FAIL MOTOR_BRIDGE");
                $finish;
            end else if (op == "W") begin
                rc = $fscanf(cf, "%h %h", a, d);
                resp = 2'b00;
                if (a < 32'h100) begin
                    axi_write(a[5:0], d, 4'hF, 0, 0, 0, resp);
                    if (resp != 2'b00) $fatal(1, "driver write 0x%02h=0x%08h got SLVERR", a, d);
                end else if (a == 32'h100) begin
                    @(negedge clk); mcmd = d; repeat (GPIO_LAT) @(posedge clk);
                end else if (a == 32'h200) begin
                    @(negedge clk); inj = d[4:0]; @(posedge clk);
                end else if (a == 32'h204) begin
                    for (k = 0; k < d; k = k + 1) @(posedge clk);
                end else $fatal(1, "bad write address 0x%h", a);
                n_wr = n_wr + 1;
                $fwrite(rf, "B %0d\n", resp);
                $fflush(rf);
            end else if (op == "R") begin
                rc = $fscanf(cf, "%h", a);
                resp = 2'b00;
                if (a < 32'h100) begin
                    axi_read(a[5:0], 0, d, resp);
                    if (resp != 2'b00) $fatal(1, "driver read 0x%02h got SLVERR", a);
                end else if (a == 32'h108) begin
                    repeat (GPIO_LAT) @(posedge clk); @(negedge clk); d = mst;
                end else $fatal(1, "bad read address 0x%h", a);
                n_rd = n_rd + 1;
                $fwrite(rf, "D %h %0d\n", d, resp);
                $fflush(rf);
            end else begin
                $fatal(1, "unknown command %0s", op);
            end
        end
    end
endmodule
part03/ch30/host/motor_cosim_main.c · c · 원본 · PC 시험 통과 2026-11-19 · 보드 ⚠ 미검증
/* (추가) 30장: PC 협동 시뮬레이션 main. NPU(0x00)·모터 GPIO(0x100)·시험 주입(0x200) 접근은 22장 npu_io_cosim.c가
 * FIFO로 Icarus 브리지(tb_p3_motor_bridge)에 전달한다. UART는 표준 입출력(29장 sensor_io_host.c).
 */
#include <stdio.h>
#include "motor_app.h"

void cosim_open(const char *cmd_path, const char *rsp_path);
void cosim_close(void);

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    int n;
    if (argc != 3) {
        fprintf(stderr, "usage: %s <cmd fifo> <rsp fifo>\n", argv[0]);
        return 2;
    }
    cosim_open(argv[1], argv[2]);
    n = motor_app_run(0x0, 0x100, 0x200);
    cosim_close();
    fprintf(stderr, "C_MOTOR_APP_EXIT responses=%d\n", n);
    return 0;
}
part03/ch30/reference/motor_reference.py · python · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""30장: NPU 추천 + 독립 모터 안전 코어 기준모델(정본, 표준 라이브러리만). 교육용이며 기능안전 인증 로직이 아니다.

구조(가이드 1절): 센서 창 → 29장 guard·NPU·경보 정책(펌웨어) → 추천(방향·속도) → AXI GPIO 명령 워드
                → p3_motor_safety(명령 승인·워치독 → 독립 인터록 → 방향·데드타임·램프·PWM) → IN1/IN2/ENA
NPU·CPU는 명령 워드만 쓴다. 핀은 코어만 구동하며 E-Stop·고장·과열·워치독·트립·리밋은 AI 점수와 무관하게 우선한다.

추천 규칙(펌웨어 recommend): 29장 정책 상태 NORMAL → 운전자 방향·80 %, WARNING → 운전자 방향·30 %,
                             FAULT·UNKNOWN → STOP. 운전자 방향은 'D' 명령(기본 FWD).
코어 클록 규칙은 rtl/p3_motor_safety.v 머리말과 같다. Core.step()은 (현재 상태·입력의 조합 출력) 후 클록 갱신.
"""
import argparse
import random
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]))

STOP, FWD, REV = 0, 1, 2
L_ESTOP, L_FAULT, L_OTEMP, L_WD, L_RESET = 1, 2, 4, 8, 16
NORMAL, WARNING, FAULT, UNKNOWN = 0, 1, 2, 3
DUTY_OF_STATE = {NORMAL: 80, WARNING: 30, FAULT: 0, UNKNOWN: 0}
BOARD = dict(PWM_PERIOD=5000, DEAD_CYC=10_000_000, WD_CYC=50_000_000, RAMP_CYC=100_000)
COSIM = dict(PWM_PERIOD=200, DEAD_CYC=3000, WD_CYC=400_000, RAMP_CYC=40)
UNIT = dict(PWM_PERIOD=100, DEAD_CYC=9, WD_CYC=70, RAMP_CYC=3)


def cmd_word(d, duty, kick, clr=0):
    return (duty & 0x7F) | ((d & 3) << 8) | ((clr & 1) << 12) | ((kick & 1) << 15)


class Core:
    def __init__(self, PWM_PERIOD, DEAD_CYC, WD_CYC, RAMP_CYC):
        self.P, self.DEAD, self.WD, self.RAMP = PWM_PERIOD, DEAD_CYC, WD_CYC, RAMP_CYC
        assert self.P % 100 == 0
        self.reset()

    def reset(self):
        self.kick_q = self.clr_q = self.cmd_err = 0
        self.req_dir = STOP
        self.req_duty = self.wd_cnt = 0
        self.latched = L_RESET
        self.drive_dir = STOP
        self.dead = self.ramp = self.pwm = self.duty = 0

    def comb(self, cmd, estop, fault, otemp, lim_f, lim_r):
        wd_exp = int(self.wd_cnt == 0)
        trip = int(self.latched != 0)
        hard = estop or fault or otemp or wd_exp or trip
        lim_block = (self.drive_dir == FWD and lim_f) or (self.drive_dir == REV and lim_r)
        gate = int(not hard and not lim_block)
        in1 = int(gate and self.drive_dir == FWD)
        in2 = int(gate and self.drive_dir == REV)
        ena = int((in1 or in2) and self.pwm < self.duty * (self.P // 100))
        status = (self.duty | self.cmd_err << 7 | self.drive_dir << 8 | self.req_dir << 10 | gate << 12
                  | int(self.dead != 0) << 13 | trip << 14 | wd_exp << 15 | self.latched << 16
                  | estop << 21 | fault << 22 | otemp << 23 | lim_f << 24 | lim_r << 25
                  | in1 << 26 | in2 << 27 | ena << 28)
        return dict(in1=in1, in2=in2, ena=ena, status=status, gate=gate, wd_exp=wd_exp, trip=trip, hard=hard)

    def step(self, cmd, estop=0, fault=0, otemp=0, lim_f=0, lim_r=0, rst=0):
        if rst:
            self.reset()
            return None
        o = self.comb(cmd, estop, fault, otemp, lim_f, lim_r)
        kick_ev = ((cmd >> 15) & 1) ^ self.kick_q
        clr_ev = ((cmd >> 12) & 1) ^ self.clr_q
        d, u = (cmd >> 8) & 3, cmd & 0x7F
        ok = d != 3 and u <= 100
        # 인터록 래치(현재 req_dir·wd_cnt 기준)
        cause = (estop * L_ESTOP) | (fault * L_FAULT) | (otemp * L_OTEMP) | (o["wd_exp"] * L_WD)
        nl = self.latched | cause
        if clr_ev and self.req_dir == STOP:
            nl = cause | (self.latched & cause)
        # 구동(현재 레지스터 기준)
        req_ok = not o["hard"] and not (self.req_dir == FWD and lim_f) and not (self.req_dir == REV and lim_r)
        dd, dead, ramp, duty = self.drive_dir, self.dead, self.ramp, self.duty
        if not req_ok or self.req_dir == STOP or (dd != STOP and dd != self.req_dir):
            duty, ramp = 0, 0
            if dd != STOP:
                dd, dead = STOP, self.DEAD
            elif dead:
                dead -= 1
        elif dd == STOP:
            ramp = 0
            if dead:
                dead -= 1
            else:
                dd = self.req_dir
        elif self.req_duty < duty:
            duty, ramp = self.req_duty, 0
        elif self.req_duty > duty:
            if ramp == self.RAMP - 1:
                duty, ramp = duty + 1, 0
            else:
                ramp += 1
        else:
            ramp = 0
        # 명령 승인·워치독
        if kick_ev:
            self.wd_cnt = self.WD
            self.cmd_err = int(not ok)
            self.req_dir = d if ok else STOP
            self.req_duty = u if ok and d != STOP else 0
        elif self.wd_cnt:
            self.wd_cnt -= 1
        self.kick_q, self.clr_q = (cmd >> 15) & 1, (cmd >> 12) & 1
        self.latched = nl
        self.drive_dir, self.dead, self.ramp, self.duty = dd, dead, ramp, duty
        self.pwm = 0 if self.pwm == self.P - 1 else self.pwm + 1
        return o


class Invariant:
    """클록마다 확인하는 안전 성질(코어 출력과 입력만 본다)."""

    def __init__(self, dead):
        self.dead, self.off_run, self.last_dir, self.viol, self.since_reset_clear = dead, 10 ** 9, 0, [], False

    def check(self, n, o, estop, fault, otemp, lim_f, lim_r, trip, wd_exp):
        on = o["in1"] or o["in2"] or o["ena"]
        if on and (estop or fault or otemp or trip or wd_exp):
            self.viol.append((n, "output while hard stop"))
        if o["in1"] and o["in2"]:
            self.viol.append((n, "in1 and in2"))
        if (o["in1"] and lim_f) or (o["in2"] and lim_r):
            self.viol.append((n, "drive into limit"))
        if o["ena"] and not (o["in1"] or o["in2"]):
            self.viol.append((n, "ena without direction"))
        d = FWD if o["in1"] else REV if o["in2"] else 0
        if d:
            if self.last_dir and d != self.last_dir and self.off_run < self.dead:
                self.viol.append((n, f"reverse after {self.off_run} off cycles"))
            self.last_dir, self.off_run = d, 0
        else:
            self.off_run += 1


def unit_vectors(out, n_rand=120000, seed=3001):
    """RTL 코어 벡터: rst cmd estop fault otemp lim_f lim_r | in1 in2 ena status (UNIT 파라미터)."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    c = Core(**UNIT)
    rows, kick, clr = [], 0, 0
    inv = Invariant(UNIT["DEAD_CYC"])
    ins = dict(estop=0, fault=0, otemp=0, lim_f=0, lim_r=0)
    cmd = 0

    def emit(rst=0, n=1):
        for _ in range(n):
            o = c.step(cmd, rst=rst, **ins)
            if o is None:
                rows.append(f"1 {cmd:08x} 0 0 0 0 0 0 0 0 00000000")
                continue
            inv.check(len(rows), o, trip=o["trip"], wd_exp=o["wd_exp"], **ins)
            rows.append(f"0 {cmd:08x} {ins['estop']} {ins['fault']} {ins['otemp']} {ins['lim_f']} {ins['lim_r']} "
                        f"{o['in1']} {o['in2']} {o['ena']} {o['status']:08x}")

    def send(d, duty, clear=False, n=1):
        nonlocal cmd, kick, clr
        kick ^= 1
        cmd = cmd_word(d, duty, kick, clr)
        emit(n=n)
        if clear:                                   # kick 다음 쓰기에서 clear(같은 클록 WD 재장전은 다음 클록부터)
            clr ^= 1
            cmd = cmd_word(d, duty, kick, clr)
            emit(n=n)

    emit(rst=1, n=2)
    emit(n=5)                                       # Reset 뒤: 명령 없어도 출력 0
    send(FWD, 50, n=3)                              # 해제 전 FWD: 트립 유지
    send(STOP, 0, clear=True, n=3)                  # 해제
    send(FWD, 60, n=40)                             # 데드타임(0) → FWD, 램프
    send(FWD, 20, n=10)                             # 즉시 감속
    send(REV, 40, n=40)                             # 반전: DEAD 동안 off
    ins["estop"] = 1
    emit(n=5)                                       # 조합 차단 + 래치
    ins["estop"] = 0
    send(REV, 40, clear=True, n=5)                  # 운전 명령 중 해제 요청 → 거부(트립 유지)
    send(STOP, 0, clear=True, n=3)
    send(REV, 40, n=30)
    ins["lim_r"] = 1
    emit(n=5)                                       # 진행 방향 리밋: 차단(래치 없음)
    send(FWD, 30, n=30)                             # 반대 방향은 허용
    ins["lim_r"] = 0
    emit(n=UNIT["WD_CYC"] + 5)                      # kick 없음 → 워치독 만료·래치
    send(FWD, 30, n=5)
    send(STOP, 0, clear=True, n=3)
    send(FWD, 101, n=5)                             # 무효 명령 → STOP + cmd_err
    send(3, 10, n=5)
    for name in ("fault", "otemp"):
        send(FWD, 70, n=20)
        ins[name] = 1
        emit(n=3)
        ins[name] = 0
        send(STOP, 0, clear=True, n=3)
    active, left, last = None, 0, (FWD, 70)                            # 무작위 운전: 명령·짧은 고장·트립 뒤 STOP+해제·워치독 공백·Reset
    for _ in range(n_rand):
        r = rng.random()
        if active and left == 0:
            ins[active], active = 0, None
        if r < 0.0005:
            emit(rst=1)
        elif r < 0.001:
            emit(n=UNIT["WD_CYC"] + rng.randint(1, 10))
        elif r < 0.004 and active is None:
            active, left = rng.choice(list(ins)), rng.randint(1, 25)
            ins[active] = 1
            emit()
        elif c.latched and r < 0.06:
            send(STOP, 0, clear=True)
        elif r < 0.03:
            send(*last)                                 # 같은 명령 재전송(kick = 워치독 갱신)
        elif r < 0.034:
            last = (rng.choice([STOP, FWD, FWD, FWD, REV, REV, 3]), rng.choice([0, 15, 40, 70, 100, 100, 101]))
            send(*last, clear=rng.random() < 0.1)
        else:
            emit()
        left = max(0, left - 1)
    out.write_text("\n".join(rows) + "\n")
    ena_on = sum(1 for r in rows if r.split()[0] == "0" and r.split()[9] == "1")
    print(f"MOTOR_UNIT_VECTORS n={len(rows)} ena_on={ena_on} invariant_violations={len(inv.viol)}")
    assert not inv.viol, inv.viol[:5]


def selftest():
    c = Core(**UNIT)
    for _ in range(5):
        assert c.step(cmd_word(FWD, 50, 0))["in1"] == 0           # Reset 뒤 해제 전에는 움직이지 않는다
    assert c.latched & L_RESET
    # 가이드 4절 예제와 같은 우선순위: estop·fault·데이터 끊김(fresh 아님)이면 출력 0
    c = Core(**UNIT)
    c.step(cmd_word(STOP, 0, 1))
    c.step(cmd_word(STOP, 0, 1, 1))
    for _ in range(3 + UNIT["DEAD_CYC"]):
        c.step(cmd_word(FWD, 80, 0, 1))
    seen = [c.step(cmd_word(FWD, 80, 0, 1)) for _ in range(20)]
    assert any(o["in1"] for o in seen)
    assert c.step(cmd_word(FWD, 80, 0, 1), estop=1)["in1"] == 0
    assert c.step(cmd_word(FWD, 80, 0, 1))["in1"] == 0              # 래치: 풀려도 해제 전까지 0
    print("MOTOR_REFERENCE_SELFTEST_PASS")


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--selftest", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--unit-vectors", type=Path)
    a = p.parse_args()
    if a.selftest:
        selftest()
    if a.unit_vectors:
        unit_vectors(a.unit_vectors)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

/* (추가) 31장: 교육용 AES-128(암호화 방향)·CTR, SHA-256, HMAC-SHA-256, 일정시간 비교. 부채널 대책 없음. */
#ifndef P3_CRYPTO_H
#define P3_CRYPTO_H
#include <stdint.h>

void aes128_encrypt_block(const uint8_t key[16], const uint8_t in[16], uint8_t out[16]);
/* CTR(SP 800-38A): iv의 마지막 32비트(BE)를 블록마다 1 증가. 암호화·복호가 같은 연산 */
void aes128_ctr(const uint8_t key[16], const uint8_t iv[16], const uint8_t *in, uint8_t *out, uint32_t n);

typedef struct {
    uint32_t h[8];
    uint64_t len;
    uint8_t buf[64];
    uint32_t fill;
} sha256_t;
void sha256_init(sha256_t *s);
void sha256_update(sha256_t *s, const uint8_t *p, uint32_t n);
void sha256_final(sha256_t *s, uint8_t out[32]);
/* key ≤ 64바이트. 메시지를 두 조각(a, b)으로 받아 복사 없이 계산 */
void hmac_sha256(const uint8_t *key, uint32_t klen, const uint8_t *a, uint32_t na, const uint8_t *b, uint32_t nb,
                 uint8_t out[32]);
/* 일정시간 비교: 모든 바이트를 끝까지 비교하고 차이를 OR로 모은다(앞에서 멈추지 않음). 같으면 1 */
int ct_equal(const uint8_t *a, const uint8_t *b, uint32_t n);

part03/ch31/sw/p3_crypto.c · c · 원본 · PC 시험 통과 2026-11-19 · 보드 ⚠ 미검증
/* (추가) 31장: 교육용 암호 구현. S-box 표는 reference/secure_reference.py(SBOX 계산)에서 생성했다.
 * 정답 확인: NIST FIPS 197·SP 800-38A·FIPS 180-4 예제, RFC 4231 HMAC — host/crypto_selftest.c와 협동 시뮬레이션.
 */
#include "p3_crypto.h"

static const uint8_t sbox[256] = {
    0x63,0x7c,0x77,0x7b,0xf2,0x6b,0x6f,0xc5,0x30,0x01,0x67,0x2b,0xfe,0xd7,0xab,0x76,
    0xca,0x82,0xc9,0x7d,0xfa,0x59,0x47,0xf0,0xad,0xd4,0xa2,0xaf,0x9c,0xa4,0x72,0xc0,
    0xb7,0xfd,0x93,0x26,0x36,0x3f,0xf7,0xcc,0x34,0xa5,0xe5,0xf1,0x71,0xd8,0x31,0x15,
    0x04,0xc7,0x23,0xc3,0x18,0x96,0x05,0x9a,0x07,0x12,0x80,0xe2,0xeb,0x27,0xb2,0x75,
    0x09,0x83,0x2c,0x1a,0x1b,0x6e,0x5a,0xa0,0x52,0x3b,0xd6,0xb3,0x29,0xe3,0x2f,0x84,
    0x53,0xd1,0x00,0xed,0x20,0xfc,0xb1,0x5b,0x6a,0xcb,0xbe,0x39,0x4a,0x4c,0x58,0xcf,
    0xd0,0xef,0xaa,0xfb,0x43,0x4d,0x33,0x85,0x45,0xf9,0x02,0x7f,0x50,0x3c,0x9f,0xa8,
    0x51,0xa3,0x40,0x8f,0x92,0x9d,0x38,0xf5,0xbc,0xb6,0xda,0x21,0x10,0xff,0xf3,0xd2,
    0xcd,0x0c,0x13,0xec,0x5f,0x97,0x44,0x17,0xc4,0xa7,0x7e,0x3d,0x64,0x5d,0x19,0x73,
    0x60,0x81,0x4f,0xdc,0x22,0x2a,0x90,0x88,0x46,0xee,0xb8,0x14,0xde,0x5e,0x0b,0xdb,
    0xe0,0x32,0x3a,0x0a,0x49,0x06,0x24,0x5c,0xc2,0xd3,0xac,0x62,0x91,0x95,0xe4,0x79,
    0xe7,0xc8,0x37,0x6d,0x8d,0xd5,0x4e,0xa9,0x6c,0x56,0xf4,0xea,0x65,0x7a,0xae,0x08,
    0xba,0x78,0x25,0x2e,0x1c,0xa6,0xb4,0xc6,0xe8,0xdd,0x74,0x1f,0x4b,0xbd,0x8b,0x8a,
    0x70,0x3e,0xb5,0x66,0x48,0x03,0xf6,0x0e,0x61,0x35,0x57,0xb9,0x86,0xc1,0x1d,0x9e,
    0xe1,0xf8,0x98,0x11,0x69,0xd9,0x8e,0x94,0x9b,0x1e,0x87,0xe9,0xce,0x55,0x28,0xdf,
    0x8c,0xa1,0x89,0x0d,0xbf,0xe6,0x42,0x68,0x41,0x99,0x2d,0x0f,0xb0,0x54,0xbb,0x16
};

static uint8_t xtime(uint8_t a) { return (uint8_t)((a << 1) ^ ((a & 0x80u) ? 0x1Bu : 0u)); }

void aes128_encrypt_block(const uint8_t key[16], const uint8_t in[16], uint8_t out[16])
{
    uint8_t rk[16], s[16], t[16], tmp[4], rcon = 1, a0, a1, a2, a3, x;
    int r, i, c;
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
        rk[i] = key[i];
        s[i] = in[i] ^ key[i];
    }
    for (r = 1; r <= 10; r++) {
        tmp[0] = (uint8_t)(sbox[rk[13]] ^ rcon); tmp[1] = sbox[rk[14]]; tmp[2] = sbox[rk[15]]; tmp[3] = sbox[rk[12]];
        for (i = 0; i < 16; i++)                       /* 다음 라운드 키(즉석 생성) */
            rk[i] ^= (i < 4) ? tmp[i] : rk[i - 4];
        rcon = xtime(rcon);
        for (i = 0; i < 16; i++)                       /* SubBytes + ShiftRows */
            t[i] = sbox[s[(i + 4 * (i % 4)) % 16]];
        for (c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
            a0 = t[4 * c]; a1 = t[4 * c + 1]; a2 = t[4 * c + 2]; a3 = t[4 * c + 3];
            if (r != 10) {                             /* MixColumns */
                x = a0 ^ a1 ^ a2 ^ a3;
                t[4 * c]     = a0 ^ x ^ xtime(a0 ^ a1);
                t[4 * c + 1] = a1 ^ x ^ xtime(a1 ^ a2);
                t[4 * c + 2] = a2 ^ x ^ xtime(a2 ^ a3);
                t[4 * c + 3] = a3 ^ x ^ xtime(a3 ^ a0);
            }
        }
        for (i = 0; i < 16; i++)
            s[i] = t[i] ^ rk[i];
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++)
        out[i] = s[i];
}

void aes128_ctr(const uint8_t key[16], const uint8_t iv[16], const uint8_t *in, uint8_t *out, uint32_t n)
{
    uint8_t cb[16], ks[16];
    uint32_t i, k, ctr = ((uint32_t)iv[12] << 24) | ((uint32_t)iv[13] << 16) | ((uint32_t)iv[14] << 8) | iv[15];
    for (i = 0; i < 12; i++)
        cb[i] = iv[i];
    for (i = 0; i < n; i += 16) {
        cb[12] = (uint8_t)(ctr >> 24); cb[13] = (uint8_t)(ctr >> 16); cb[14] = (uint8_t)(ctr >> 8); cb[15] = (uint8_t)ctr;
        aes128_encrypt_block(key, cb, ks);
        for (k = 0; k < 16 && i + k < n; k++)
            out[i + k] = in[i + k] ^ ks[k];
        ctr++;
    }
}

static const uint32_t K[64] = {
    0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2};

#define ROR(x, n) (((x) >> (n)) | ((x) << (32 - (n))))

static void sha256_block(uint32_t h[8], const uint8_t *p)
{
    uint32_t w[64], a, b, c, d, e, f, g, hh, t1, t2;
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++)
        w[i] = ((uint32_t)p[4 * i] << 24) | ((uint32_t)p[4 * i + 1] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[4 * i + 2] << 8) | p[4 * i + 3];
    for (i = 16; i < 64; i++)
        w[i] = (ROR(w[i - 2], 17) ^ ROR(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >> 10)) + w[i - 7]
               + (ROR(w[i - 15], 7) ^ ROR(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >> 3)) + w[i - 16];
    a = h[0]; b = h[1]; c = h[2]; d = h[3]; e = h[4]; f = h[5]; g = h[6]; hh = h[7];
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
        t1 = hh + (ROR(e, 6) ^ ROR(e, 11) ^ ROR(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[i] + w[i];
        t2 = (ROR(a, 2) ^ ROR(a, 13) ^ ROR(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c));
        hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = d + t1; d = c; c = b; b = a; a = t1 + t2;
    }
    h[0] += a; h[1] += b; h[2] += c; h[3] += d; h[4] += e; h[5] += f; h[6] += g; h[7] += hh;
}

void sha256_init(sha256_t *s)
{
    static const uint32_t iv[8] = {0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
                                   0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19};
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++)
        s->h[i] = iv[i];
    s->len = 0;
    s->fill = 0;
}

void sha256_update(sha256_t *s, const uint8_t *p, uint32_t n)
{
    uint32_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        s->buf[s->fill++] = p[i];
        if (s->fill == 64) {
            sha256_block(s->h, s->buf);
            s->fill = 0;
        }
    }
    s->len += n;
}

void sha256_final(sha256_t *s, uint8_t out[32])
{
    uint64_t bits = s->len * 8u;
    uint8_t pad = 0x80, z = 0, lb[8];
    int i;
    sha256_update(s, &pad, 1);
    while (s->fill != 56)
        sha256_update(s, &z, 1);
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++)
        lb[i] = (uint8_t)(bits >> (56 - 8 * i));
    sha256_update(s, lb, 8);
    for (i = 0; i < 32; i++)
        out[i] = (uint8_t)(s->h[i / 4] >> (24 - 8 * (i % 4)));
}

void hmac_sha256(const uint8_t *key, uint32_t klen, const uint8_t *a, uint32_t na, const uint8_t *b, uint32_t nb,
                 uint8_t out[32])
{
    uint8_t k0[64], inner[32];
    sha256_t s;
    uint32_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++)
        k0[i] = (uint8_t)((i < klen ? key[i] : 0) ^ 0x36);
    sha256_init(&s);
    sha256_update(&s, k0, 64);
    sha256_update(&s, a, na);
    sha256_update(&s, b, nb);
    sha256_final(&s, inner);
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++)
        k0[i] ^= 0x36 ^ 0x5C;
    sha256_init(&s);
    sha256_update(&s, k0, 64);
    sha256_update(&s, inner, 32);
    sha256_final(&s, out);
}

int ct_equal(const uint8_t *a, const uint8_t *b, uint32_t n)
{
    uint32_t i;
    uint8_t d = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++)
        d |= a[i] ^ b[i];
    return d == 0;
}
