/* (추가) 31장: 보안 UART 모델 적재 응용. 규격은 reference/secure_reference.py 머리말. */
#ifndef SECURE_APP_H
#define SECURE_APP_H
#include <stdint.h>
int secure_app_run(uintptr_t npu_base);   /* PC 빌드는 입력이 끝나면 처리 수를 돌려준다 */
#endif
part03/ch31/sw/secure_app.c · c · 원본 · PC 시험 통과 2026-11-19 · 보드 ⚠ 미검증
/* (추가) 31장: 보안 UART 모델 적재 + 추론. 규격의 정본은 reference/secure_reference.py 머리말.
 *   'M' 보안 모델 프레임: CRC16(전송) → ver → len ≤ 256 → HMAC 검증(일정시간) → seq > last_seq → iv 결속
 *       → last_seq 소비 → AES-CTR 복호(스테이징 뱅크) → 모델 검사 → 원자적 커밋(활성 뱅크 전환 + NPU Bias 재적재)
 *       MAC 확인 전에는 복호하지 않고, 검사·커밋이 실패하면 활성 모델은 그대로다.
 *   'S' 추론(29장 센서 창, guard·특징 재사용): 프레임의 model_id가 활성 모델과 같아야 한다.
 *   'I' 식별(활성 모델·키 지문·last_seq). 키 값은 출력하지 않는다.
 * 키는 build/secure_keys.h(저장소 제외)에만 있다. 교육용: Basys3에는 키 보호 저장소·Secure Boot·Tamper 방어가 없다.
 * last_seq는 RAM에만 있어 Reset 뒤 0으로 돌아간다(재부팅 뒤 Replay 가능 — 알려진 한계, README 참조).
 */
#include "app.h"
#include "npu_driver.h"
#include "sensor_app.h"
#include "sensor_io.h"
#include "secure_keys.h"
#include "p3_crypto.h"
#include "secure_app.h"

#define MAX_LEN   256u
#define HDR_LEN   27u                 /* ver 1 + seq 4 + model_id 4 + iv 16 + len 2 */
#define BLOB_LEN  54u
#define FND_ON(m) ((uint32_t)(m) << 16)

enum { R_VERSION = 1, R_LENGTH, R_AUTH, R_REPLAY, R_NONCE, R_MODEL, R_COMMIT };
static const char *const rname[] = {"", "VERSION", "LENGTH", "AUTH", "REPLAY", "NONCE", "MODEL", "COMMIT"};

typedef struct {
    uint32_t id;
    int8_t w[SENSOR_N_CLS][SENSOR_N_FEAT];
    int64_t b[SENSOR_N_CLS];
} model_bank_t;

static model_bank_t bank[2];          /* bank[active] = 사용 중, bank[!active] = 스테이징 */
static int active;
static int8_t w_exp[NPU_N_CLASS][NPU_N_IN];   /* 활성 뱅크에서 만든 20장 IP 형태(나머지 0) */
static int64_t b_exp[NPU_N_CLASS];
static uint32_t last_seq;
static int last_reject;
static uint8_t rx[HDR_LEN + MAX_LEN + 32], plain[MAX_LEN];
static int8_t x_buf[NPU_N_IN];
static char score_txt[3 * 16];

static uint32_t rd32(const uint8_t *p) { return p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24); }

static uint32_t crc32_byte(uint32_t crc, uint8_t v)
{
    int b;
    crc ^= v;
    for (b = 0; b < 8; b++)
        crc = (crc & 1u) ? (crc >> 1) ^ 0xEDB88320u : crc >> 1;
    return crc;
}

/* 29장 MODEL_ID 규칙: 20장 IP 형태 w[10][784] ‖ b[10](int64 LE)의 CRC32. 빈 칸은 0, 쓰지 않는 Bias는 -2^38 */
static uint32_t bank_id(const model_bank_t *m)
{
    uint32_t crc = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    int c, i;
    for (c = 0; c < NPU_N_CLASS; c++)
        for (i = 0; i < NPU_N_IN; i++)
            crc = crc32_byte(crc, (uint8_t)(c < SENSOR_N_CLS && i < SENSOR_N_FEAT ? m->w[c][i] : 0));
    for (c = 0; c < NPU_N_CLASS; c++) {
        uint64_t v = (uint64_t)(c < SENSOR_N_CLS ? m->b[c] : -((int64_t)1 << 38));
        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++)
            crc = crc32_byte(crc, (uint8_t)(v >> (8 * i)));
    }
    return ~crc;
}

/* 평문 blob → 뱅크(스테이징 검사). 성공 1 */
static int parse_blob(const uint8_t *p, uint32_t n, model_bank_t *m)
{
    int c, i;
    if (n != BLOB_LEN || p[0] != 'P' || p[1] != '3' || p[2] != 'M' || p[3] != 'D')
        return 0;
    if ((p[4] | (p[5] << 8)) != 1 || p[6] != SENSOR_N_CLS || p[7] != SENSOR_N_FEAT)
        return 0;
    for (c = 0; c < SENSOR_N_CLS; c++)
        for (i = 0; i < SENSOR_N_FEAT; i++) {
            m->w[c][i] = (int8_t)p[12 + SENSOR_N_FEAT * c + i];
            if (m->w[c][i] == -128)                          /* 대칭 양자화 범위 -127..127 */
                return 0;
        }
    for (c = 0; c < SENSOR_N_CLS; c++) {
        uint64_t v = 0;
        for (i = 7; i >= 0; i--)
            v = (v << 8) | p[30 + 8 * c + i];
        m->b[c] = (int64_t)v;
        if (m->b[c] < -((int64_t)1 << 39) || m->b[c] >= ((int64_t)1 << 39))   /* 20장 Bias 40비트 */
            return 0;
    }
    m->id = bank_id(m);
    return m->id == rd32(p + 8);
}

static void expand(const model_bank_t *m)
{
    int c, i;
    for (c = 0; c < NPU_N_CLASS; c++) {
        for (i = 0; i < SENSOR_N_FEAT; i++)
            w_exp[c][i] = c < SENSOR_N_CLS ? m->w[c][i] : 0;
        b_exp[c] = c < SENSOR_N_CLS ? m->b[c] : -((int64_t)1 << 38);
    }
}

static void show(void)
{
    uint32_t fnd = ((bank[active].id >> 16) & 0xFFFFu) | FND_ON(0xF);
    uint32_t led = (uint32_t)active | (last_reject ? (1u << 14) : 0) | (SECURE_TEST_KEY ? (1u << 13) : 0);
    sio_display(fnd, led);
    APP_PRINTF("DISP fnd=%08x led=%04x\r\n", (unsigned)fnd, (unsigned)led);
    sio_flush();
}

static void reject(int r, uint32_t seq)
{
    last_reject = r;
    APP_PRINTF("SECURE result=REJECT reason=%s code=%d seq=%d last_seq=%d active=%08x\r\n", rname[r], r, (int)seq,
               (int)last_seq, (unsigned)bank[active].id);
    show();
}

static void error_line(const char *name, int code, int detail)
{
    APP_PRINTF("ERROR code=%d name=%s detail=%d active=%08x\r\n", code, name, detail, (unsigned)bank[active].id);
    show();
}

/* rx = 헤더 + 암호문 + 태그(CRC 통과). 처리 순서가 규격이다. */
static void secure_load(npu_t *npu, uint32_t len)
{
    uint8_t tag[32];
    const uint8_t *iv = rx + 9;
    uint32_t seq = rd32(rx + 1), mid = rd32(rx + 5), ivseq, ivctr;
    int st = !active, rc;
    if (rx[0] != 1) {
        reject(R_VERSION, seq);
        return;
    }
    hmac_sha256(mac_key, 32, (const uint8_t *)"P3SM", 4, rx, HDR_LEN + len, tag);
    if (!ct_equal(tag, rx + HDR_LEN + len, 32)) {     /* 인증 전에는 복호·시퀀스 소비 없음 */
        reject(R_AUTH, seq);
        return;
    }
    if (seq <= last_seq) {
        reject(R_REPLAY, seq);
        return;
    }
    ivseq = ((uint32_t)iv[8] << 24) | ((uint32_t)iv[9] << 16) | ((uint32_t)iv[10] << 8) | iv[11];
    ivctr = ((uint32_t)iv[12] << 24) | ((uint32_t)iv[13] << 16) | ((uint32_t)iv[14] << 8) | iv[15];
    if (ivseq != seq || ivctr != 0) {                  /* 같은 키에서 Nonce 재사용 금지: seq에 결속 */
        reject(R_NONCE, seq);
        return;
    }
    last_seq = seq;                                    /* 인증된 메시지는 내용이 틀려도 다시 쓰지 못하게 소비 */
    aes128_ctr(enc_key, iv, rx + HDR_LEN, plain, len);
    if (!parse_blob(plain, len, &bank[st]) || bank[st].id != mid) {
        reject(R_MODEL, seq);
        return;
    }
    expand(&bank[st]);                                 /* 커밋: 새 뱅크로 IP 형태를 만들고 Bias 적재 */
    rc = npu_set_bias(npu, b_exp);
    if (rc != NPU_OK) {                                /* 실패: 이전 뱅크로 되돌린다 */
        expand(&bank[active]);
        npu_set_bias(npu, b_exp);
        reject(R_COMMIT, seq);
        return;
    }
    active = st;
    last_reject = 0;
    APP_PRINTF("SECURE result=COMMIT seq=%d model=%08x bank=%d last_seq=%d\r\n", (int)seq, (unsigned)bank[active].id,
               active, (int)last_seq);
    show();
}

static char *fmt_i64(char *p, int64_t v)
{
    char tmp[20];
    int n = 0;
    uint64_t u = v < 0 ? (uint64_t)(-(v + 1)) + 1u : (uint64_t)v;
    if (v < 0)
        *p++ = '-';
    do {
        tmp[n++] = (char)('0' + (int)(u % 10u));
        u /= 10u;
    } while (u);
    while (n)
        *p++ = tmp[--n];
    return p;
}

static void infer(npu_t *npu)
{
    sensor_win_t s;
    int64_t ns[NPU_N_CLASS], cs[NPU_N_CLASS];
    const uint8_t *p = rx + 6;
    int seq = rx[0] | (rx[1] << 8), i, c, fm, um, ncls = -1, ccls = -1, rc, same = 1;
    int8_t f[SENSOR_N_FEAT];
    char *o = score_txt;
    if (rd32(rx + 2) != bank[active].id) {
        error_line("MODEL", 4, seq);
        return;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 3; i++, p += 2)
        s.age[i] = (uint16_t)(p[0] | (p[1] << 8));
    for (i = 0; i < SENSOR_WIN; i++, p += 6) {
        s.t[i] = (int16_t)(uint16_t)(p[0] | (p[1] << 8));
        s.v[i] = (uint16_t)(p[2] | (p[3] << 8));
        s.d[i] = (uint16_t)(p[4] | (p[5] << 8));
    }
    sensor_guard(&s, &fm, &um);
    if (fm || um) {
        APP_PRINTF("INFER seq=%d model=%08x guard=%s npu=-1 cpu=-1 match=-1 scores=-\r\n", seq,
                   (unsigned)bank[active].id, fm ? "FAULT" : "UNKNOWN");
        show();
        return;
    }
    sensor_features(&s, f);
    for (i = 0; i < SENSOR_N_FEAT; i++)
        x_buf[i] = f[i];
    rc = npu_infer(npu, x_buf, (const int8_t (*)[NPU_N_IN])w_exp, ns, &ncls);
    if (rc == NPU_OK) {
        npu_clear_done(npu);
        ccls = cpu_infer(x_buf, (const int8_t (*)[NPU_N_IN])w_exp, b_exp, cs);
        for (c = 0; c < NPU_N_CLASS; c++)
            same &= ns[c] == cs[c];
        same &= ncls == ccls;
    } else {
        same = 0;
    }
    for (c = 0; c < SENSOR_N_CLS; c++) {
        if (c)
            *o++ = ',';
        o = fmt_i64(o, rc == NPU_OK ? ns[c] : 0);
    }
    *o = '\0';
    APP_PRINTF("INFER seq=%d model=%08x guard=OK npu=%d cpu=%d match=%d scores=%s\r\n", seq,
               (unsigned)bank[active].id, ncls, ccls, same, score_txt);
    show();
}

static uint16_t crc16(const uint8_t *p, uint32_t n, uint16_t crc)
{
    uint32_t i;
    int b;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        crc ^= (uint16_t)p[i] << 8;
        for (b = 0; b < 8; b++)
            crc = (crc & 0x8000u) ? (uint16_t)((crc << 1) ^ 0x1021u) : (uint16_t)(crc << 1);
    }
    return crc;
}

/* n바이트를 rx+off에 받으며 CRC 누적. 실패 시 SIO_TIMEOUT/SIO_EOF */
static int recv(uint32_t off, uint32_t n, uint16_t *crc)
{
    uint32_t i;
    int c;
    uint8_t b;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        c = sio_getc(SIO_IN_FRAME);
        if (c < 0)
            return c;
        b = (uint8_t)c;
        rx[off + i] = b;
        *crc = crc16(&b, 1, *crc);
    }
    return 0;
}

static int handle(npu_t *npu)
{
    int c, prev = -1, cmd, rc;
    uint16_t crc;
    uint8_t b;
    uint32_t len = 0;
    for (;;) {
        c = sio_getc(SIO_WAIT_LINK);
        if (c == SIO_EOF)
            return -1;
        if (c == SIO_TIMEOUT)
            continue;                                  /* 31장은 링크 감시 동작 없음(대기 계속) */
        if (prev == 'P' && c == '3')
            break;
        prev = c;
    }
    cmd = sio_getc(SIO_IN_FRAME);
    if (cmd < 0) {
        error_line("TIMEOUT", 2, 0);
        return cmd == SIO_EOF ? -1 : 0;
    }
    b = (uint8_t)cmd;
    crc = crc16(&b, 1, 0xFFFFu);
    if (cmd == 'M') {
        rc = recv(0, HDR_LEN, &crc);
        if (rc == 0) {
            len = rx[25] | ((uint32_t)rx[26] << 8);
            if (len > MAX_LEN) {                       /* 버퍼 넘침 방지: 나머지는 끊길 때까지 버린다 */
                while ((c = sio_getc(SIO_IN_FRAME)) >= 0)
                    ;
                reject(R_LENGTH, rd32(rx + 1));
                return c == SIO_EOF ? -1 : 0;
            }
            rc = recv(HDR_LEN, len + 32, &crc);
        }
    } else if (cmd == 'S') {
        rc = recv(0, SENSOR_PAYLOAD, &crc);
    } else if (cmd == 'I') {
        rc = 0;
    } else {
        while ((c = sio_getc(SIO_IN_FRAME)) >= 0)
            ;
        error_line("CMD", 3, cmd);
        return c == SIO_EOF ? -1 : 0;
    }
    if (rc == 0) {
        uint8_t t[2];
        for (c = 0; c < 2 && rc == 0; c++) {
            int v = sio_getc(SIO_IN_FRAME);
            if (v < 0)
                rc = v;
            else {
                t[c] = (uint8_t)v;
                crc = crc16(&t[c], 1, crc);
            }
        }
    }
    if (rc < 0) {                                      /* 끊긴 프레임: 아무것도 반영하지 않는다 */
        error_line("TIMEOUT", 2, 0);
        return rc == SIO_EOF ? -1 : 0;
    }
    if (crc != 0) {
        error_line("CRC", 1, 0);
        return 0;
    }
    if (cmd == 'M')
        secure_load(npu, len);
    else if (cmd == 'S')
        infer(npu);
    else {
        APP_PRINTF("P3_SECURE READY model=%08x key_fp=%08x test_key=%d last_seq=%d\r\n", (unsigned)bank[active].id,
                   (unsigned)SECURE_KEY_FP, SECURE_TEST_KEY, (int)last_seq);
        sio_flush();
    }
    return 0;
}

int secure_app_run(uintptr_t npu_base)
{
    npu_t npu;
    int n = 0, ok, rc;
    npu_init(&npu, npu_base);
    active = 0;
    ok = parse_blob(model_a_blob, BLOB_LEN, &bank[0]) && bank[0].id == MODEL_A_ID;
    expand(&bank[0]);
    rc = npu_set_bias(&npu, b_exp);
    APP_PRINTF("P3_SECURE READY model=%08x model_ok=%d bias_rc=%d key_fp=%08x test_key=%d last_seq=%d\r\n",
               (unsigned)bank[0].id, ok, rc, (unsigned)SECURE_KEY_FP, SECURE_TEST_KEY, (int)last_seq);
    show();
    while (handle(&npu) == 0)
        n++;
    return n;
}
part03/ch31/host/p3_secure_host.py · python · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""31장 PC 호스트: 보안 모델 적재(Encrypt-then-MAC) + 공격·고장 시험 + 적재 전후 추론 확인 → CSV.

  --cosim "<명령>"  PC 빌드 펌웨어 + Icarus NPU RTL      --port <포트>  Basys3(28장 Bitstream + 31장 ELF)
  --key-file F     보드용 키(저장소 밖, 16진 96자). 없으면 TEST 키(펌웨어도 TEST 키로 빌드됐어야 한다)
시험: 모델 A로 추론 → 모델 B 보안 적재(COMMIT) → B로 추론(점수 = B 골든) → 거부·전송 오류 시험 17종(각각 활성 모델 B 유지)
      → 인증 실패가 시퀀스를 소비하지 않음 → A 재적재(seq 6) → A로 추론.
판정: 응답 필드와 표시(DISP)가 secure_reference.Device 기대와 같아야 한다. 추론 점수는 활성 모델의 PC 골든과 같아야 한다.
키 값은 화면·로그·CSV에 쓰지 않는다(지문만).
"""
import argparse
import csv
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]))
from part03.ch29.host.p3_sensor_host import ProcTransport, SerialTransport  # noqa: E402
from part03.ch29.reference import sensor_reference as s29  # noqa: E402
from part03.ch31.reference.secure_reference import (  # noqa: E402
    REJ, TEST_KEYS, Device, blob, key_fp, load_qmodel_b, secure_payload,
)


class Host:
    def __init__(self, tr, timeout, log, w, target, dev, test_key):
        self.tr, self.timeout, self.log, self.w, self.target, self.dev = tr, timeout, log, w, target, dev
        self.test_key, self.last_reject, self.n, self.passed, self.fails, self.step = test_key, 0, 0, 0, [], 0

    def block(self):
        lines = []
        while True:
            line = self.tr.readline(self.timeout)
            if line is None:
                return lines
            self.log.write(line + "\n")
            lines.append(line)
            if line.startswith("DISP") or (line.startswith("P3_SECURE") and len(lines) == 1):
                return lines

    def disp_expect(self):
        mid = s29.model_id(self.dev.qm)
        return ((mid >> 16) & 0xFFFF) | (0xF << 16), self.dev.bank | (1 << 14 if self.last_reject else 0) \
            | (1 << 13 if self.test_key else 0)

    def record(self, name, ok, errs, main):
        self.step += 1
        self.n += 1
        self.passed += ok
        if not ok:
            self.fails.append((self.step, name, errs))
        self.w.writerow(dict(target=self.target, step=self.step, name=name, ok=int(ok), detail=";".join(errs),
                             line=main or ""))
        print(f"STEP {self.step:3d} {name:24s} {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} {';'.join(errs) or (main or '')[:140]}")

    def check_disp(self, lines, errs):
        d = s29.parse_kv(lines[-1]) if lines and lines[-1].startswith("DISP") else None
        exp = self.disp_expect()
        if not d or (int(d["fnd"], 16), int(d["led"], 16)) != exp:
            errs.append(f"disp={d}/{exp[0]:08x},{exp[1]:04x}")

    def secure(self, name, payload, raw=None):
        """raw가 있으면 그 바이트를 보낸다(전송 변조). 기대는 Device.secure(payload)."""
        self.tr.write(raw if raw is not None else s29.frame(b"M", payload))
        e = self.dev.secure(payload)
        self.last_reject = 0 if e["result"] == "COMMIT" else REJ[e["reason"]]
        lines = self.block()
        main = next((ln for ln in lines if ln.startswith("SECURE")), None)
        kv = s29.parse_kv(main or "")
        errs = [] if main else [f"no SECURE: {lines}"]
        for k, v in e.items():
            want = f"{v:08x}" if k in ("model", "active") else str(v)
            if kv.get(k) != want:
                errs.append(f"{k}={kv.get(k)}/{want}")
        self.check_disp(lines, errs)
        self.record(name, not errs, errs, main)
        return e

    def error(self, name, raw, code, wait=0.0):
        self.tr.write(raw)
        if wait:
            time.sleep(wait)
        lines = self.block()
        main = next((ln for ln in lines if ln.startswith("ERROR")), None)
        kv = s29.parse_kv(main or "")
        errs = [] if main and kv.get("code") == str(code) else [f"want ERROR code={code}: {lines}"]
        if kv.get("active") != f"{s29.model_id(self.dev.qm):08x}":
            errs.append(f"active={kv.get('active')}")
        self.check_disp(lines, errs)
        self.record(name, not errs, errs, main)

    def infer(self, name, s, seq):
        qm = self.dev.qm
        self.tr.write(s29.sensor_frame(seq, s29.model_id(qm), s))
        lines = self.block()
        main = next((ln for ln in lines if ln.startswith("INFER")), None)
        kv = s29.parse_kv(main or "")
        sc, cls = s29.golden(s29.features(s), qm)
        errs = []
        if kv.get("model") != f"{s29.model_id(qm):08x}":
            errs.append(f"model={kv.get('model')}")
        if kv.get("scores") != ",".join(map(str, sc)) or kv.get("npu") != str(cls) or kv.get("match") != "1":
            errs.append(f"scores={kv.get('scores')}/{sc} npu={kv.get('npu')}/{cls}")
        self.check_disp(lines, errs)
        self.record(name, not errs, errs, main)
        return cls


def suite(h, keys, ds, cosim):
    qa, qb = s29.load_qmodel(), load_qmodel_b()
    ida, idb = s29.model_id(qa), s29.model_id(qb)
    pa, pb = blob(qa), blob(qb)
    nonce = iter(bytes([i]) * 8 for i in range(1, 255))
    diff = [s for s in ds if s29.golden(s29.features(s), qa)[1] != s29.golden(s29.features(s), qb)[1]][:2]
    samples = ds[:3] + diff                        # 두 모델의 class가 다른 창 포함
    for i, s in enumerate(samples):
        h.infer(f"infer_model_a_{i}", s, 10 + i)
    h.secure("load_model_b", secure_payload(keys, 1, pb, idb, next(nonce)))
    for i, s in enumerate(samples):
        h.infer(f"infer_model_b_{i}", s, 20 + i)
    good2 = secure_payload(keys, 2, pa, ida, next(nonce))
    ln = 27 + len(pa)
    h.secure("tamper_ciphertext", secure_payload(keys, 2, pa, ida, next(nonce), tamper=40))
    h.secure("tamper_tag", secure_payload(keys, 2, pa, ida, next(nonce), tamper=ln + 5))
    h.secure("tamper_header_seq", secure_payload(keys, 2, pa, ida, next(nonce), tamper=1))
    h.secure("tamper_header_model", secure_payload(keys, 2, pa, ida, next(nonce), tamper=6))
    short = bytearray(secure_payload(keys, 2, pa, ida, next(nonce)))
    short[25] -= 1                                 # 길이 필드 1 줄임(뒤 바이트가 태그로 밀림)
    h.secure("length_field_minus1", bytes(short)[:-1])
    big = secure_payload(keys, 2, bytes(300), ida, next(nonce))
    h.secure("length_over_256", big)
    h.secure("wrong_mac_key", secure_payload((keys[0], bytes(32)), 2, pa, ida, next(nonce)))
    h.secure("version_2", secure_payload(keys, 2, pa, ida, next(nonce), ver=2))
    h.secure("replay_seq1", secure_payload(keys, 1, pb, idb, next(nonce)))
    h.secure("nonce_not_bound", secure_payload(keys, 2, pa, ida, next(nonce), iv_seq=7))
    h.secure("forged_seq_1000", secure_payload((keys[0], b"\x55" * 32), 1000, pa, ida, next(nonce)))
    h.secure("wrong_enc_key_auth_ok", secure_payload((bytes(16), keys[1]), 3, pa, ida, next(nonce)))   # seq 3 소비
    h.secure("model_id_mismatch", secure_payload(keys, 4, pa, idb, next(nonce)))                     # seq 4 소비
    h.secure("replay_consumed_seq3", secure_payload(keys, 3, pa, ida, next(nonce)))
    raw = bytearray(s29.frame(b"M", good2))
    raw[50] ^= 0x10
    h.error("transport_crc", bytes(raw), 1)
    h.error("cut_mid_frame", s29.frame(b"M", good2)[:60], 2, wait=1.0 if cosim else 5.0)
    for i, s in enumerate(samples[-2:]):
        h.infer(f"still_model_b_{i}", s, 30 + i)
    bad_id = bytearray(pa)
    bad_id[8] ^= 0xFF                              # 평문 안의 model_id만 틀림(헤더는 실제 계산값) → MODEL
    h.secure("blob_internal_id_bad", secure_payload(keys, 5, bytes(bad_id), ida, next(nonce)))
    h.secure("load_model_a_seq6", secure_payload(keys, 6, pa, ida, next(nonce)))
    for i, s in enumerate(samples[-2:]):
        h.infer(f"infer_model_a_again_{i}", s, 40 + i)
    h.tr.write(s29.frame(b"I"))
    lines = h.block()
    kv = s29.parse_kv(lines[0] if lines else "")
    ok = bool(lines) and kv.get("model") == f"{ida:08x}" and kv.get("last_seq") == "6" \
        and kv.get("key_fp") == f"{key_fp(keys):08x}"
    h.record("identify", ok, [] if ok else [str(lines)], lines[0] if lines else None)


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    g = p.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    g.add_argument("--port")
    g.add_argument("--cosim")
    p.add_argument("--baud", type=int, default=115200)
    p.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=60.0)
    p.add_argument("--key-file")
    p.add_argument("--csv", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--log", type=Path, required=True)
    a = p.parse_args()
    if a.key_file:
        raw = bytes.fromhex(Path(a.key_file).read_text().strip())
        keys, test = (raw[:16], raw[16:]), 0
    else:
        keys, test = TEST_KEYS, 1
    ds = s29.dataset(*s29.TEST)[:400]
    s29.load_qmodel()
    tr = SerialTransport(a.port, a.baud) if a.port else ProcTransport(a.cosim)
    target = "fpga" if a.port else "rtl_cosim"
    dev = Device(keys, s29.load_qmodel())
    with a.log.open("w") as log, a.csv.open("w", newline="") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=["target", "step", "name", "ok", "detail", "line"])
        w.writeheader()
        h = Host(tr, a.timeout, log, w, target, dev, test)
        if a.port:
            print("보드의 btnC(Reset)를 누르세요: 부팅 줄(P3_SECURE READY)을 기다립니다")
            tr.s.reset_input_buffer()
        line = tr.readline(120 if a.port else a.timeout)
        while a.port and line is not None and not line.startswith("P3_SECURE READY"):
            line = tr.readline(120)
        log.write(f"{line}\n")
        kv = s29.parse_kv(line or "")
        ok = bool(line) and kv.get("model_ok") == "1" and kv.get("bias_rc") == "0" \
            and kv.get("key_fp") == f"{key_fp(keys):08x}" and kv.get("test_key") == str(test) and kv.get("last_seq") == "0"
        lines = h.block()
        errs = [] if ok else [f"boot={line}"]
        h.check_disp(lines, errs)
        h.record("boot", not errs, errs, line)
        suite(h, keys, ds, a.cosim is not None)
    rc = tr.close()
    print(f"SECURE_HOST_SUMMARY target={target} checks={h.passed}/{h.n} key_fp={key_fp(keys):08x} test_key={test} proc_rc={rc}")
    for step, name, errs in h.fails[:10]:
        print(f"FAIL_DETAIL step={step} {name} {';'.join(errs)}")
    ok = h.passed == h.n and rc == 0
    print("SECURE_HOST_PASS" if ok else "SECURE_HOST_FAIL")
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
part03/ch31/rtl/p3_aes128_enc.v · verilog · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
// (추가) 31장: AES-128 암호화 코어(FIPS 197, 암호화 방향만 — CTR 모드 키스트림에는 이것만 필요하다).
//   반복형: 1클록에 1라운드, 라운드 키는 즉석 생성. start 뒤 10클록에 done 1클록, result 유지.
//   바이트 순서: key/block/result[127:120]이 FIPS 197의 첫 바이트(in0). 상태는 열 우선(바이트 i = 행 i%4, 열 i/4).
//   S-box 표는 reference/secure_reference.py의 SBOX(GF(2^8) 역원 + 아핀 변환으로 계산)에서 생성했다.
//   교육용 구현: 부채널(전력·타이밍) 대책 없음.
`timescale 1ns/1ps
module p3_aes128_enc (
    input  wire         clk,
    input  wire         rst,
    input  wire         start,
    input  wire [127:0] key,
    input  wire [127:0] block,
    output reg          busy,
    output reg          done,
    output reg  [127:0] result
);
    function [7:0] sbox;
        input [7:0] x;
        case (x)
            8'h00: sbox = 8'h63;
            8'h01: sbox = 8'h7c;
            8'h02: sbox = 8'h77;

            8'hde: sbox = 8'h1d;
            8'hdf: sbox = 8'h9e;
            8'he0: sbox = 8'he1;
            8'he1: sbox = 8'hf8;
            8'he2: sbox = 8'h98;
            8'he3: sbox = 8'h11;
            8'he4: sbox = 8'h69;
            8'he5: sbox = 8'hd9;
            8'he6: sbox = 8'h8e;
            8'he7: sbox = 8'h94;
            8'he8: sbox = 8'h9b;
            8'he9: sbox = 8'h1e;
            8'hea: sbox = 8'h87;
            8'heb: sbox = 8'he9;
            8'hec: sbox = 8'hce;
            8'hed: sbox = 8'h55;
            8'hee: sbox = 8'h28;
            8'hef: sbox = 8'hdf;
            8'hf0: sbox = 8'h8c;
            8'hf1: sbox = 8'ha1;
            8'hf2: sbox = 8'h89;
            8'hf3: sbox = 8'h0d;
            8'hf4: sbox = 8'hbf;
            8'hf5: sbox = 8'he6;
            8'hf6: sbox = 8'h42;
            8'hf7: sbox = 8'h68;
            8'hf8: sbox = 8'h41;
            8'hf9: sbox = 8'h99;
            8'hfa: sbox = 8'h2d;
            8'hfb: sbox = 8'h0f;
            8'hfc: sbox = 8'hb0;
            8'hfd: sbox = 8'h54;
            8'hfe: sbox = 8'hbb;
            8'hff: sbox = 8'h16;
        endcase
    endfunction

    function [7:0] xtime;
        input [7:0] a;
        xtime = {a[6:0], 1'b0} ^ (a[7] ? 8'h1b : 8'h00);
    endfunction

    function [127:0] sub_shift;               // SubBytes + ShiftRows
        input [127:0] s;
        integer i, j;
        begin
            for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1) begin
                j = (i + 4 * (i % 4)) % 16;
                sub_shift[127 - 8 * i -: 8] = sbox(s[127 - 8 * j -: 8]);
            end
        end
    endfunction

    function [127:0] mix;                     // MixColumns
        input [127:0] s;
        integer c, i;
        reg [7:0] a [0:3];
        reg [7:0] t;
        begin
            for (c = 0; c < 4; c = c + 1) begin
                for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) a[i] = s[127 - 8 * (4 * c + i) -: 8];
                t = a[0] ^ a[1] ^ a[2] ^ a[3];
                for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1)
                    mix[127 - 8 * (4 * c + i) -: 8] = a[i] ^ t ^ xtime(a[i] ^ a[(i + 1) % 4]);
            end
        end
    endfunction

    function [127:0] next_key;
        input [127:0] k;
        input [7:0] rc;
        reg [31:0] w0, w1, w2, w3, t;
        begin
            {w0, w1, w2, w3} = k;
            t = {sbox(w3[23:16]) ^ rc, sbox(w3[15:8]), sbox(w3[7:0]), sbox(w3[31:24])};
            w0 = w0 ^ t; w1 = w1 ^ w0; w2 = w2 ^ w1; w3 = w3 ^ w2;
            next_key = {w0, w1, w2, w3};
        end
    endfunction

    reg [127:0] st, rk;
    reg [7:0]   rcon;
    reg [3:0]   round;
    wire [127:0] rk_n = next_key(rk, rcon);
    wire [127:0] ss   = sub_shift(st);
    wire [127:0] st_n = (round == 4'd10 ? ss : mix(ss)) ^ rk_n;

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (rst) begin
            busy <= 1'b0; done <= 1'b0; round <= 4'd0; rcon <= 8'h01; st <= 128'd0; rk <= 128'd0; result <= 128'd0;
        end else begin
            done <= 1'b0;
            if (start && !busy) begin
                st <= block ^ key; rk <= key; rcon <= 8'h01; round <= 4'd1; busy <= 1'b1;
            end else if (busy) begin
                st <= st_n; rk <= rk_n; rcon <= xtime(rcon);
                if (round == 4'd10) begin
                    busy <= 1'b0; done <= 1'b1; result <= st_n;
                end
                round <= round + 4'd1;
            end
        end
    end
endmodule
part03/ch31/rtl/p3_sha256_core.v · verilog · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
// (추가) 31장: SHA-256 압축 코어(FIPS 180-4). 반복형: 1클록에 1라운드, 블록당 64클록 + 1.
//   init = IV로 새 메시지의 첫 블록, next = 이전 결과에 이어 다음 블록. 패딩은 호출 쪽(펌웨어·TB)이 한다.
//   block[511:480] = 첫 32비트 워드(big-endian). digest[255:224] = H0. done 1클록, digest 유지.
//   HMAC-SHA-256은 이 코어를 두 번(내부·외부 해시) 쓰는 메시지 단위 구성이다(FIPS 198-1). K 표는 처음 64개 소수
//   세제곱근의 소수부로 계산해 확인했다. 교육용 구현: 부채널 대책 없음.
`timescale 1ns/1ps
module p3_sha256_core (
    input  wire         clk,
    input  wire         rst,
    input  wire         init,
    input  wire         next,
    input  wire [511:0] block,
    output reg          busy,
    output reg          done,
    output wire [255:0] digest
);
    function [31:0] kc;
        input [5:0] t;
        case (t)
            6'd0: kc = 32'h428a2f98;
            6'd1: kc = 32'h71374491;
            6'd2: kc = 32'hb5c0fbcf;
            6'd3: kc = 32'he9b5dba5;
            6'd4: kc = 32'h3956c25b;
            6'd5: kc = 32'h59f111f1;
            6'd6: kc = 32'h923f82a4;
            6'd7: kc = 32'hab1c5ed5;
            6'd8: kc = 32'hd807aa98;
            6'd9: kc = 32'h12835b01;
            6'd10: kc = 32'h243185be;
            6'd11: kc = 32'h550c7dc3;
            6'd12: kc = 32'h72be5d74;
            6'd13: kc = 32'h80deb1fe;
            6'd14: kc = 32'h9bdc06a7;
            6'd15: kc = 32'hc19bf174;
            6'd16: kc = 32'he49b69c1;
            6'd17: kc = 32'hefbe4786;
            6'd18: kc = 32'h0fc19dc6;
            6'd19: kc = 32'h240ca1cc;
            6'd20: kc = 32'h2de92c6f;
            6'd21: kc = 32'h4a7484aa;
            6'd22: kc = 32'h5cb0a9dc;
            6'd23: kc = 32'h76f988da;
            6'd24: kc = 32'h983e5152;
            6'd25: kc = 32'ha831c66d;
            6'd26: kc = 32'hb00327c8;
            6'd27: kc = 32'hbf597fc7;
            6'd28: kc = 32'hc6e00bf3;
            6'd29: kc = 32'hd5a79147;
            6'd30: kc = 32'h06ca6351;
            6'd31: kc = 32'h14292967;
            6'd32: kc = 32'h27b70a85;
            6'd33: kc = 32'h2e1b2138;
            6'd34: kc = 32'h4d2c6dfc;
            6'd35: kc = 32'h53380d13;
            6'd36: kc = 32'h650a7354;
            6'd37: kc = 32'h766a0abb;
            6'd38: kc = 32'h81c2c92e;
            6'd39: kc = 32'h92722c85;
            6'd40: kc = 32'ha2bfe8a1;
            6'd41: kc = 32'ha81a664b;
            6'd42: kc = 32'hc24b8b70;
            6'd43: kc = 32'hc76c51a3;
            6'd44: kc = 32'hd192e819;
            6'd45: kc = 32'hd6990624;
            6'd46: kc = 32'hf40e3585;
            6'd47: kc = 32'h106aa070;
            6'd48: kc = 32'h19a4c116;
            6'd49: kc = 32'h1e376c08;
            6'd50: kc = 32'h2748774c;
            6'd51: kc = 32'h34b0bcb5;
            6'd52: kc = 32'h391c0cb3;
            6'd53: kc = 32'h4ed8aa4a;
            6'd54: kc = 32'h5b9cca4f;
            6'd55: kc = 32'h682e6ff3;
            6'd56: kc = 32'h748f82ee;
            6'd57: kc = 32'h78a5636f;
            6'd58: kc = 32'h84c87814;
            6'd59: kc = 32'h8cc70208;
            6'd60: kc = 32'h90befffa;
            6'd61: kc = 32'ha4506ceb;
            6'd62: kc = 32'hbef9a3f7;
            6'd63: kc = 32'hc67178f2;
        endcase
    endfunction
    function [31:0] rotr;
        input [31:0] x;
        input integer n;
        rotr = (x >> n) | (x << (32 - n));
    endfunction

    reg [31:0] H [0:7];
    reg [31:0] a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h;
    reg [31:0] w [0:15];
    reg [6:0]  t;
    integer i;
    wire [31:0] s1 = rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25);
    wire [31:0] ch = (e & f) ^ (~e & g);
    wire [31:0] t1 = h + s1 + ch + kc(t[5:0]) + w[0];
    wire [31:0] s0 = rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22);
    wire [31:0] mj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c);
    wire [31:0] t2 = s0 + mj;
    wire [31:0] ws0 = rotr(w[1], 7) ^ rotr(w[1], 18) ^ (w[1] >> 3);
    wire [31:0] ws1 = rotr(w[14], 17) ^ rotr(w[14], 19) ^ (w[14] >> 10);
    wire [31:0] w_new = ws1 + w[9] + ws0 + w[0];
    assign digest = {H[0], H[1], H[2], H[3], H[4], H[5], H[6], H[7]};

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (rst) begin
            busy <= 1'b0; done <= 1'b0; t <= 7'd0;
            H[0] <= 32'h6a09e667; H[1] <= 32'hbb67ae85; H[2] <= 32'h3c6ef372; H[3] <= 32'ha54ff53a;
            H[4] <= 32'h510e527f; H[5] <= 32'h9b05688c; H[6] <= 32'h1f83d9ab; H[7] <= 32'h5be0cd19;
        end else begin
            done <= 1'b0;
            if ((init || next) && !busy) begin
                if (init) begin
                    a <= 32'h6a09e667; b <= 32'hbb67ae85; c <= 32'h3c6ef372; d <= 32'ha54ff53a;
                    e <= 32'h510e527f; f <= 32'h9b05688c; g <= 32'h1f83d9ab; h <= 32'h5be0cd19;
                    H[0] <= 32'h6a09e667; H[1] <= 32'hbb67ae85; H[2] <= 32'h3c6ef372; H[3] <= 32'ha54ff53a;
                    H[4] <= 32'h510e527f; H[5] <= 32'h9b05688c; H[6] <= 32'h1f83d9ab; H[7] <= 32'h5be0cd19;
                end else begin
                    a <= H[0]; b <= H[1]; c <= H[2]; d <= H[3]; e <= H[4]; f <= H[5]; g <= H[6]; h <= H[7];
                end
                for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1) w[i] <= block[511 - 32 * i -: 32];
                t <= 7'd0;
                busy <= 1'b1;
            end else if (busy && t < 7'd64) begin
                h <= g; g <= f; f <= e; e <= d + t1; d <= c; c <= b; b <= a; a <= t1 + t2;
                for (i = 0; i < 15; i = i + 1) w[i] <= w[i + 1];
                w[15] <= w_new;
                t <= t + 7'd1;
            end else if (busy) begin
                H[0] <= H[0] + a; H[1] <= H[1] + b; H[2] <= H[2] + c; H[3] <= H[3] + d;
                H[4] <= H[4] + e; H[5] <= H[5] + f; H[6] <= H[6] + g; H[7] <= H[7] + h;
                busy <= 1'b0; done <= 1'b1;
            end
        end
    end
endmodule

part03/ch31/tb/tb_p3_aes128.sv · systemverilog · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
// (추가) 31장: AES-128 코어 시험. FIPS 197 부록 C.1·B, SP 800-38A F.5.1 CTR 키스트림 블록, 무작위 200개(Python 정본)
`timescale 1ns/1ps
module tb_p3_aes128;
    reg clk = 0, rst = 1, start = 0;
    reg [127:0] key, blk, exp;
    wire busy, done;
    wire [127:0] res;
    always #5 clk = ~clk;
    p3_aes128_enc dut(.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .start(start), .key(key), .block(blk), .busy(busy), .done(done), .result(res));
    reg [8*256-1:0] vpath;
    integer f, r, n = 0, errors = 0, cyc, max_cyc = 0;
    initial begin
        if (!$value$plusargs("VEC=%s", vpath)) $fatal(1, "+VEC required");
        f = $fopen(vpath, "r");
        repeat (3) @(posedge clk);
        rst = 0;
        while (!$feof(f)) begin
            r = $fscanf(f, "%h %h %h\n", key, blk, exp);
            if (r == 3) begin
                @(negedge clk); start = 1; @(negedge clk); start = 0;
                cyc = 1;
                while (!done) begin @(posedge clk); #1; cyc = cyc + 1; end
                if (cyc > max_cyc) max_cyc = cyc;
                if (res !== exp) begin
                    errors = errors + 1;
                    if (errors <= 5) $display("MISMATCH n=%0d key=%h pt=%h got %h exp %h", n, key, blk, res, exp);
                end
                n = n + 1;
            end
        end
        if (errors == 0 && n >= 6) $display("RTL_SIM_PASS AES128 blocks=%0d kat=6 cycles_per_block=%0d", n, max_cyc);
        else $display("RTL_SIM_FAIL AES128 blocks=%0d errors=%0d", n, errors);
        $finish;
    end
endmodule
part03/ch31/tb/tb_p3_sha256.sv · systemverilog · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
// (추가) 31장: SHA-256 압축 코어 시험. FIPS 180-4 예제("abc", 448비트), RFC 4231 HMAC 1·2의 내부·외부 해시 메시지,
//   빈 메시지·경계 길이·무작위 메시지(Python이 패딩한 블록). 메시지마다 init → next … 순서로 넣고 최종 digest 대조.
`timescale 1ns/1ps
module tb_p3_sha256;
    reg clk = 0, rst = 1, init = 0, next = 0;
    reg [511:0] blk;
    wire busy, done;
    wire [255:0] dg;
    always #5 clk = ~clk;
    p3_sha256_core dut(.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .init(init), .next(next), .block(blk), .busy(busy), .done(done), .digest(dg));
    reg [8*256-1:0] vpath;
    reg [8*8-1:0] tag;
    reg [255:0] exp;
    integer f, r, nb, k, n = 0, blocks = 0, errors = 0, cyc;
    initial begin
        if (!$value$plusargs("VEC=%s", vpath)) $fatal(1, "+VEC required");
        f = $fopen(vpath, "r");
        repeat (3) @(posedge clk);
        rst = 0;
        while (!$feof(f)) begin
            r = $fscanf(f, "%s %d %h\n", tag, nb, exp);
            if (r == 3) begin
                for (k = 0; k < nb; k = k + 1) begin
                    r = $fscanf(f, "%h\n", blk);
                    @(negedge clk); init = (k == 0); next = (k != 0); @(negedge clk); init = 0; next = 0;
                    cyc = 1;
                    while (!done) begin @(posedge clk); #1; cyc = cyc + 1; end
                    blocks = blocks + 1;
                end
                if (dg !== exp) begin
                    errors = errors + 1;
                    if (errors <= 5) $display("MISMATCH msg=%0d blocks=%0d got %h exp %h", n, nb, dg, exp);
                end
                n = n + 1;
            end
        end
        if (errors == 0 && n > 5) $display("RTL_SIM_PASS SHA256 messages=%0d blocks=%0d cycles_per_block=%0d", n, blocks, cyc);
        else $display("RTL_SIM_FAIL SHA256 messages=%0d errors=%0d", n, errors);
        $finish;
    end
endmodule
part03/ch31/host/secure_cosim_main.c · c · 원본 · PC 시험 통과 2026-11-19 · 보드 ⚠ 미검증
/* (추가) 31장: PC 협동 시뮬레이션 main(22장 FIFO 브리지 → 20장 NPU RTL, UART = 표준 입출력). */
#include <stdio.h>
#include "secure_app.h"

void cosim_open(const char *cmd_path, const char *rsp_path);
void cosim_close(void);

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    int n;
    if (argc != 3) {
        fprintf(stderr, "usage: %s <cmd fifo> <rsp fifo>\n", argv[0]);
        return 2;
    }
    cosim_open(argv[1], argv[2]);
    n = secure_app_run(0);
    cosim_close();
    fprintf(stderr, "C_SECURE_APP_EXIT responses=%d\n", n);
    return 0;
}
part03/ch31/host/crypto_selftest.c · c · 원본 · PC 시험 통과 2026-11-19 · 보드 ⚠ 미검증
/* (추가) 31장: C 암호 구현 알려진 정답 시험(PC). FIPS 197 C.1, SP 800-38A F.5.1(2블록), FIPS 180-4 "abc"·448비트,
 * RFC 4231 HMAC 1·2. 모두 맞으면 C_CRYPTO_KAT_PASS. */
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "p3_crypto.h"

static void hex(const char *s, uint8_t *o) { while (*s) { sscanf(s, "%2hhx", o++); s += 2; } }

static int check(const char *name, const uint8_t *got, const char *exp_hex, int n)
{
    uint8_t e[64];
    hex(exp_hex, e);
    if (memcmp(got, e, (size_t)n)) { printf("C_CRYPTO_KAT_FAIL %s\n", name); return 1; }
    return 0;
}

int main(void)
{
    uint8_t k[32], p[64], o[64], iv[16];
    sha256_t s;
    int f = 0;
    hex("000102030405060708090a0b0c0d0e0f", k); hex("00112233445566778899aabbccddeeff", p);
    aes128_encrypt_block(k, p, o); f |= check("aes_c1", o, "69c4e0d86a7b0430d8cdb78070b4c55a", 16);
    hex("2b7e151628aed2a6abf7158809cf4f3c", k); hex("f0f1f2f3f4f5f6f7f8f9fafbfcfdfeff", iv);
    hex("6bc1bee22e409f96e93d7e117393172aae2d8a571e03ac9c9eb76fac45af8e51", p);
    aes128_ctr(k, iv, p, o, 32);
    f |= check("ctr_f51", o, "874d6191b620e3261bef6864990db6ce9806f66b7970fdff8617187bb9fffdff", 32);
    sha256_init(&s); sha256_update(&s, (const uint8_t *)"abc", 3); sha256_final(&s, o);
    f |= check("sha_abc", o, "ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad", 32);
    sha256_init(&s);
    sha256_update(&s, (const uint8_t *)"abcdbcdecdefdefgefghfghighijhijkijkljklmklmnlmnomnopnopq", 56);
    sha256_final(&s, o);
    f |= check("sha_448", o, "248d6a61d20638b8e5c026930c3e6039a33ce45964ff2167f6ecedd419db06c1", 32);
    memset(k, 0x0b, 20);
    hmac_sha256(k, 20, (const uint8_t *)"Hi ", 3, (const uint8_t *)"There", 5, o);
    f |= check("hmac_4231_1", o, "b0344c61d8db38535ca8afceaf0bf12b881dc200c9833da726e9376c2e32cff7", 32);
    hmac_sha256((const uint8_t *)"Jefe", 4, (const uint8_t *)"what do ya want for nothing?", 28, NULL, 0, o);
    f |= check("hmac_4231_2", o, "5bdcc146bf60754e6a042426089575c75a003f089d2739839dec58b964ec3843", 32);
    f |= !ct_equal(o, o, 32) || ct_equal((const uint8_t *)"ab", (const uint8_t *)"ac", 2);
    if (!f) printf("C_CRYPTO_KAT_PASS aes=1 ctr=1 sha=2 hmac=2\n");
    return f;
}
part03/ch31/reference/secure_reference.py · python · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""31장: 보안 모델 적재(Encrypt-then-MAC: AES-128-CTR + HMAC-SHA-256) 기준모델(정본, 표준 라이브러리만).
교육용 구현이다. 상용 보안 제품이 아니며 Basys3는 키 보호 저장소·Secure Boot·Tamper 방어를 제공하지 않는다.

역할 구분: AES-128(FIPS 197)·CTR(SP 800-38A) = 기밀성만, HMAC-SHA-256(FIPS 198-1) = 키 기반 무결성·인증.
SHA-256 Digest만으로는 인증이 아니고, AES-CTR만으로는 변조를 막지 못한다. HMAC은 Replay를 막지 못하므로 시퀀스를 따로 둔다.

키: 암호화 키 16바이트와 MAC 키 32바이트를 분리. 저장소에는 결정적으로 만든 **TEST 키**만 있다(TEST_KEYS).
    보드용 키는 저장소 밖 파일(P3_KEY_FILE: 16진 48바이트)에서 build/ 헤더로 만든다. 로그에는 지문만:
    key_fp = SHA-256("P3KF" ‖ enc ‖ mac)[0:4] (big-endian u32)
모델 평문(blob, 54바이트): "P3MD" ‖ ver u16=1 ‖ n_cls u8=3 ‖ n_feat u8=6 ‖ model_id u32 ‖ w[3][6] int8 ‖ b[3] int64
    model_id는 29장 규칙(20장 IP 형태로 늘린 w[10][784] ‖ b[10]의 CRC32)
보안 프레임('M' 명령 payload, 정수는 LE):
    ver u8=1 ‖ seq u32 ‖ model_id u32 ‖ iv 16 ‖ len u16 ‖ ct[len] ‖ tag 32
    iv = nonce64 ‖ seq u32(BE) ‖ ctr u32(BE)=0   (CTR 카운터는 마지막 32비트 BE 증가, SP 800-38A)
    tag = HMAC-SHA-256(mac_key, "P3SM" ‖ ver ‖ seq ‖ model_id ‖ iv ‖ len ‖ ct)   (헤더 전체 + 암호문)
장치 처리 순서(이 순서가 규격이다): CRC16(전송) → ver → len ≤ 256 → **MAC(일정시간 비교)** → seq > last_seq(Replay)
    → iv의 seq·ctr 결속(Nonce 재사용 금지) → last_seq ← seq(소비) → 복호 → 스테이징 검사(len·magic·차원·model_id·Bias 범위)
    → **원자적 커밋**(활성 뱅크 전환 + NPU Bias 재적재, 실패 시 이전 모델 유지). MAC 확인 전에는 복호하지 않는다.
거부 코드: 1 VERSION, 2 LENGTH, 3 AUTH, 4 REPLAY, 5 NONCE, 6 MODEL, 7 COMMIT(NPU Bias 적재 실패)
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import struct
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]))
from part03.ch18.reference.precision_reference import quantize_model  # noqa: E402
from part03.ch29.reference import sensor_reference as s29  # noqa: E402

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
MODEL_B_JSON = HERE / "model_b_f32.json"
MAX_LEN = 256
BLOB_LEN = 54
MAC_LABEL = b"P3SM"
REJ = {"VERSION": 1, "LENGTH": 2, "AUTH": 3, "REPLAY": 4, "NONCE": 5, "MODEL": 6, "COMMIT": 7}
TEST_KEYS = (hashlib.sha256(b"P3-CH31-TEST-ENC-KEY").digest()[:16], hashlib.sha256(b"P3-CH31-TEST-MAC-KEY").digest())

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- AES-128 (FIPS 197, 암호화 방향만: CTR에는 이것만 필요)
SBOX = [0] * 256


def _init_sbox():
    p = q = 1
    while True:                                  # GF(2^8) 역원과 아핀 변환(FIPS 197 5.1.1)
        p = p ^ ((p << 1) & 0xFF) ^ (0x1B if p & 0x80 else 0)
        q ^= q << 1
        q ^= q << 2
        q ^= q << 4
        q &= 0xFF
        if q & 0x80:
            q ^= 0x09
        x = q ^ (q << 1 | q >> 7) ^ (q << 2 | q >> 6) ^ (q << 3 | q >> 5) ^ (q << 4 | q >> 4)
        SBOX[p] = (x ^ 0x63) & 0xFF
        if p == 1:
            break
    SBOX[0] = 0x63


_init_sbox()


def xtime(a):
    return ((a << 1) ^ (0x1B if a & 0x80 else 0)) & 0xFF


def key_expand(key):
    w = [list(key[4 * i:4 * i + 4]) for i in range(4)]
    rcon = 1
    for i in range(4, 44):
        t = list(w[i - 1])
        if i % 4 == 0:
            t = [SBOX[b] for b in t[1:] + t[:1]]
            t[0] ^= rcon
            rcon = xtime(rcon)
        w.append([a ^ b for a, b in zip(w[i - 4], t)])
    return [sum(w[4 * r:4 * r + 4], []) for r in range(11)]


def aes128_encrypt_block(key, block):
    rk = key_expand(key)
    s = [b ^ k for b, k in zip(block, rk[0])]
    for r in range(1, 11):
        s = [SBOX[b] for b in s]
        s = [s[(i + 4 * (i % 4)) % 16] for i in range(16)]        # ShiftRows(열 우선 상태)
        if r != 10:
            m = []
            for c in range(4):
                a = s[4 * c:4 * c + 4]
                t = a[0] ^ a[1] ^ a[2] ^ a[3]
                m += [a[i] ^ t ^ xtime(a[i] ^ a[(i + 1) % 4]) for i in range(4)]
            s = m
        s = [b ^ k for b, k in zip(s, rk[r])]
    return bytes(s)


def ctr_xcrypt(key, iv, data):
    out, ctr = bytearray(), int.from_bytes(iv, "big")
    for i in range(0, len(data), 16):
        cb = ((ctr & ~0xFFFFFFFF) | ((ctr + i // 16) & 0xFFFFFFFF)).to_bytes(16, "big")
        ks = aes128_encrypt_block(key, cb)
        out += bytes(a ^ b for a, b in zip(data[i:i + 16], ks))
    return bytes(out)


def key_fp(keys):
    return int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(b"P3KF" + keys[0] + keys[1]).digest()[:4], "big")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 모델 blob
def load_qmodel_b():
    qm = quantize_model(json.loads(MODEL_B_JSON.read_text()), 8)
    w = [row + [0] * (s29.N_IN - s29.N_FEAT) for row in qm["w"]] + [[0] * s29.N_IN for _ in range(s29.N_CLASS - s29.N_CLS)]
    return dict(qm, w=w, b=list(qm["b"]) + [s29.UNUSED_BIAS] * (s29.N_CLASS - s29.N_CLS))


def blob(qm):
    w = bytes(v & 0xFF for row in qm["w"][:s29.N_CLS] for v in row[:s29.N_FEAT])
    out = b"P3MD" + struct.pack("<HBBI", 1, s29.N_CLS, s29.N_FEAT, s29.model_id(qm)) + w
    out += b"".join(struct.pack("<q", v) for v in qm["b"][:s29.N_CLS])
    assert len(out) == BLOB_LEN
    return out


def parse_blob(p):
    """장치와 같은 스테이징 검사. 반환 qm(20장 형태) 또는 None."""
    if len(p) != BLOB_LEN or p[:4] != b"P3MD":
        return None
    ver, nc, nf, mid = struct.unpack_from("<HBBI", p, 4)
    if ver != 1 or nc != s29.N_CLS or nf != s29.N_FEAT:
        return None
    w = [list(struct.unpack_from("<6b", p, 12 + 6 * c)) + [0] * (s29.N_IN - 6) for c in range(3)]
    b = list(struct.unpack_from("<3q", p, 30))
    if any(not -(1 << 39) <= v < (1 << 39) for v in b) or any(v == -128 for row in w for v in row):
        return None
    qm = dict(w=w + [[0] * s29.N_IN for _ in range(7)], b=b + [s29.UNUSED_BIAS] * 7)
    return qm if s29.model_id(qm) == mid else None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 프레임
def secure_payload(keys, seq, plaintext, model_id, nonce64, iv_seq=None, ctr0=0, ver=1, length=None, tamper=None):
    iv = nonce64 + struct.pack(">II", seq if iv_seq is None else iv_seq, ctr0)
    ct = ctr_xcrypt(keys[0], iv, plaintext)
    hdr = struct.pack("<BII", ver, seq, model_id) + iv + struct.pack("<H", len(ct) if length is None else length)
    tag = hmac.new(keys[1], MAC_LABEL + hdr + ct, hashlib.sha256).digest()
    p = bytearray(hdr + ct + tag)
    if tamper is not None:
        p[tamper] ^= 0x01
    return bytes(p)


def secure_frame(*a, **k):
    return s29.frame(b"M", secure_payload(*a, **k))


class Device:
    """장치 기대 상태: last_seq, 활성 모델."""

    def __init__(self, keys, qm):
        self.keys, self.qm, self.last_seq, self.bank = keys, qm, 0, 0

    def secure(self, payload):
        ver, seq, mid = struct.unpack_from("<BII", payload, 0)
        iv = payload[9:25]
        ln = struct.unpack_from("<H", payload, 25)[0]
        if ver != 1:
            return self._rej("VERSION", seq)
        if ln > MAX_LEN or len(payload) != 27 + ln + 32:
            return self._rej("LENGTH", seq)
        ct, tag = payload[27:27 + ln], payload[27 + ln:]
        if not hmac.compare_digest(tag, hmac.new(self.keys[1], MAC_LABEL + payload[:27 + ln], hashlib.sha256).digest()):
            return self._rej("AUTH", seq)
        if seq <= self.last_seq:
            return self._rej("REPLAY", seq)
        if struct.unpack(">II", iv[8:16]) != (seq, 0):
            return self._rej("NONCE", seq)
        self.last_seq = seq
        qm = parse_blob(ctr_xcrypt(self.keys[0], iv, ct))
        if qm is None or s29.model_id(qm) != mid:
            return self._rej("MODEL", seq)
        self.qm, self.bank = qm, self.bank ^ 1
        return dict(result="COMMIT", seq=seq, model=s29.model_id(qm), bank=self.bank, last_seq=self.last_seq)

    def _rej(self, reason, seq):
        return dict(result="REJECT", reason=reason, code=REJ[reason], seq=seq, last_seq=self.last_seq,
                    active=s29.model_id(self.qm))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 시험 벡터
KAT_AES = [("000102030405060708090a0b0c0d0e0f", "00112233445566778899aabbccddeeff", "69c4e0d86a7b0430d8cdb78070b4c55a"),
           ("2b7e151628aed2a6abf7158809cf4f3c", "3243f6a8885a308d313198a2e0370734", "3925841d02dc09fbdc118597196a0b32")]
KAT_CTR = ("2b7e151628aed2a6abf7158809cf4f3c", "f0f1f2f3f4f5f6f7f8f9fafbfcfdfeff",
           "6bc1bee22e409f96e93d7e117393172aae2d8a571e03ac9c9eb76fac45af8e51",
           "874d6191b620e3261bef6864990db6ce9806f66b7970fdff8617187bb9fffdff")
KAT_SHA = [(b"abc", "ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad"),
           (b"abcdbcdecdefdefgefghfghighijhijkijkljklmklmnlmnomnopnopq",
            "248d6a61d20638b8e5c026930c3e6039a33ce45964ff2167f6ecedd419db06c1")]
KAT_HMAC = [(b"\x0b" * 20, b"Hi There", "b0344c61d8db38535ca8afceaf0bf12b881dc200c9833da726e9376c2e32cff7"),
            (b"Jefe", b"what do ya want for nothing?", "5bdcc146bf60754e6a042426089575c75a003f089d2739839dec58b964ec3843")]


def sha_blocks(msg):
    """SHA-256 패딩 후 512비트 블록 목록(RTL 압축 코어 입력)."""
    m = msg + b"\x80" + b"\x00" * ((55 - len(msg)) % 64) + struct.pack(">Q", 8 * len(msg))
    return [m[i:i + 64] for i in range(0, len(m), 64)]


def rtl_vectors(aes_out, sha_out, n_rand=200, seed=3101):
    import random
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    rows = [(bytes.fromhex(k), bytes.fromhex(p), bytes.fromhex(c)) for k, p, c in KAT_AES]
    key, ivb = bytes.fromhex(KAT_CTR[0]), int(KAT_CTR[1], 16)
    for i in range(2):                                           # CTR 키스트림 블록(SP 800-38A F.5.1)
        cb = (ivb + i).to_bytes(16, "big")
        rows.append((key, cb, aes128_encrypt_block(key, cb)))
    for _ in range(n_rand):
        k, p = rng.randbytes(16), rng.randbytes(16)
        rows.append((k, p, aes128_encrypt_block(k, p)))
    aes_out.write_text("".join(f"{k.hex()} {p.hex()} {c.hex()}\n" for k, p, c in rows))
    msgs = [m for m, _ in KAT_SHA] + [b"", rng.randbytes(64), rng.randbytes(119), rng.randbytes(200)]
    for k, d, _ in KAT_HMAC:                                     # HMAC = SHA-256 두 번(RTL은 압축 코어만)
        k0 = k.ljust(64, b"\x00")
        inner = bytes(a ^ 0x36 for a in k0) + d
        msgs += [inner, bytes(a ^ 0x5C for a in k0) + hashlib.sha256(inner).digest()]
    for _ in range(40):
        msgs.append(rng.randbytes(rng.randint(0, 300)))
    lines = []
    for m in msgs:
        bl = sha_blocks(m)
        lines.append(f"M {len(bl)} {hashlib.sha256(m).hexdigest()}")
        lines += [b.hex() for b in bl]
    sha_out.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    print(f"CRYPTO_RTL_VECTORS aes={len(rows)} sha_msgs={len(msgs)} sha_blocks={sum(len(sha_blocks(m)) for m in msgs)}")


def write_header(out, key_file=None):
    if key_file:
        raw = bytes.fromhex(Path(key_file).read_text().strip())
        assert len(raw) == 48, "키 파일: 16진 96자(암호화 16바이트 ‖ MAC 32바이트)"
        keys, test = (raw[:16], raw[16:]), 0
    else:
        keys, test = TEST_KEYS, 1
    qa = s29.load_qmodel()
    lines = ["/* 자동 생성: part03/ch31/reference/secure_reference.py — 키가 들어 있으므로 저장소에 넣지 않는다(build/). */",
             "#ifndef SECURE_KEYS_H", "#define SECURE_KEYS_H", "#include <stdint.h>",
             f"#define SECURE_TEST_KEY {test}", f"#define SECURE_KEY_FP 0x{key_fp(keys):08x}u",
             "static const uint8_t enc_key[16] = {" + ",".join(f"0x{b:02x}" for b in keys[0]) + "};",
             "static const uint8_t mac_key[32] = {" + ",".join(f"0x{b:02x}" for b in keys[1]) + "};",
             "/* 부팅 모델 A(29장 모델과 같음) */",
             f"#define MODEL_A_ID 0x{s29.model_id(qa):08x}u",
             "static const uint8_t model_a_blob[54] = {" + ",".join(str(b) for b in blob(qa)) + "};",
             "#endif", ""]
    out.write_text("\n".join(lines))
    print(f"SECURE_HEADER_DONE test_key={test} key_fp={key_fp(keys):08x} model_a={s29.model_id(qa):08x}")


def selftest():
    for k, p, c in KAT_AES:
        assert aes128_encrypt_block(bytes.fromhex(k), bytes.fromhex(p)).hex() == c
    k, iv, pt, ct = KAT_CTR
    assert ctr_xcrypt(bytes.fromhex(k), bytes.fromhex(iv), bytes.fromhex(pt)).hex() == ct
    for m, d in KAT_SHA:
        assert hashlib.sha256(m).hexdigest() == d
    for k, m, d in KAT_HMAC:
        assert hmac.new(k, m, hashlib.sha256).hexdigest() == d
    cross = "cryptography_not_installed"
    try:                                                         # 독립 구현과 교차 확인(있으면)
        import os
        from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers import Cipher, algorithms, modes
        for _ in range(50):
            k, iv, d = os.urandom(16), os.urandom(12) + b"\xff\xff\xff\xf0", os.urandom(100)
            e = Cipher(algorithms.AES(k), modes.CTR(iv)).encryptor()
            ref = e.update(d) + e.finalize()
            mine = ctr_xcrypt(k, iv, d[:64])                     # 이 장의 카운터는 하위 32비트에서 감싼다: 감싸기 전 4블록만 비교
            assert ref[:64] == mine
        cross = "cryptography_match_50"
    except ImportError:
        pass
    qa, qb = s29.load_qmodel(), load_qmodel_b()
    assert parse_blob(blob(qa)) is not None and s29.model_id(parse_blob(blob(qb))) == s29.model_id(qb)
    assert s29.model_id(qa) != s29.model_id(qb)
    dev = Device(TEST_KEYS, qa)
    n = b"\x11" * 8
    good = secure_payload(TEST_KEYS, 5, blob(qb), s29.model_id(qb), n)
    assert dev.secure(secure_payload(TEST_KEYS, 5, blob(qb), s29.model_id(qb), n, tamper=40))["reason"] == "AUTH"
    assert dev.last_seq == 0                                     # 인증 실패는 시퀀스를 소비하지 않는다
    assert dev.secure(good)["result"] == "COMMIT" and dev.secure(good)["reason"] == "REPLAY"
    wrong = (TEST_KEYS[0], b"\x00" * 32)
    assert dev.secure(secure_payload(wrong, 6, blob(qa), s29.model_id(qa), n))["reason"] == "AUTH"
    assert dev.secure(secure_payload(TEST_KEYS, 6, blob(qa), s29.model_id(qa), n, iv_seq=5))["reason"] == "NONCE"
    print(f"SECURE_REFERENCE_SELFTEST_PASS aes_kat=2 ctr_kat=1 sha_kat=2 hmac_kat=2 cross={cross} "
          f"model_a={s29.model_id(qa):08x} model_b={s29.model_id(qb):08x} test_key_fp={key_fp(TEST_KEYS):08x}")


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--selftest", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--header", type=Path)
    p.add_argument("--key-file", help="보드용 키 파일(저장소 밖). 없으면 TEST 키")
    p.add_argument("--rtl-vectors", nargs=2, type=Path, metavar=("AES", "SHA"))
    a = p.parse_args()
    if a.selftest:
        selftest()
    if a.header:
        write_header(a.header, a.key_file)
    if a.rtl_vectors:
        rtl_vectors(*a.rtl_vectors)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
part03/ch31/reference/train_model_b.py · python · 원본 · RTL_SIM 통과 2026-11-19
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""31장: 보안 적재 시험용 '모델 B'(29장과 같은 6→3 구조, 다른 seed·학습 길이). PC 전용, PyTorch 필요.
29장 train_sensor_model.py를 seed·epoch만 바꿔 실행하고 결과를 이 폴더의 model_b_f32.json으로 저장한다."""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]))
import part03.ch29.reference.train_sensor_model as t  # noqa: E402

out = Path(__file__).with_name("model_b_f32.json")
t.SEED, t.EPOCHS = 3131, 700
t.MODEL_JSON = out                     # 29장 파일은 건드리지 않는다
t.main()
doc = json.loads(out.read_text())
doc["model"] = "sensor_linear_6x3_f32_model_b"
out.write_text(json.dumps(doc) + "\n")

08 장 시연

5장 비트스트림 하나에 ELF만 바꿔 6-1(공통)과 고른 응용을 차례로 시연합니다. 아래는 6-1 + 6-3 + 6-5 조합의 점검표 항목입니다. 기대값은 협동 시뮬레이션·빌드에서 얻은 값이고, 실물 실행은 아직 하지 않았습니다.
